Method: 提出一个涉及 J=1 和 J=2 转动态的手性分子 ESST 方案。该方案基于三束线偏振微波场与一束激光共同驱动的对映体特异泵浦：将一个对映体泵浦至 J=1,2 子流形内的对映体特异暗态，而另一对映体则被耗散性地泵出该流形。

Result: 在所设计的多场驱动方案下，可实现对手性分子的对映体选择性态转移：一种对映体被捕获于对映体特异暗态，另一种对映体被耗散泵出目标子流形，从而实现两类对映体的态分离。

Conclusion: 该方案为涉及更高 J 转动态的手性分子调控提供了可行途径，并可进一步推广至具有更高角动量的转动态体系，有望推动对映体检测与分离的发展。

Abstract: Enantiomer-specific state transfer (ESST) based on electric-dipole transitions has attracted considerable interest in the studies of manipulating molecules with electromagnetic fields. It also holds promise as a powerful tool for enantiomer detection and separation. To date, experimental demonstrations of ESST have focused mainly on molecules in the lowest rotational angular-momentum states, namely, $J=0$ and $J=1$. Realistic molecular samples, however, may contain appreciable populations in higher-$J$ rotational states, making it desirable to develop ESST schemes applicable to such states. Nevertheless, one cannot derive such schemes via a simple and straightforward extension of existing low-$J$ schemes, because higher-$J$ manifolds contain more magnetic sub-levels and involve more complicated networks of electric-dipole transitions. Here, we propose an ESST scheme for chiral molecules involving the $J=1$ and $J=2$ rotational states. Specifically, our scheme is based on enantiomer-specific pumping driven by three linearly polarized microwave fields and one laser beam. Under this scheme, one enantiomer is pumped into an enantiomer-specific dark state within a sub manifold with $J=1,2$, whereas the other enantiomer is dissipatively pumped out of this manifold. Our strategy can be extended to systems involving rotational states with even higher angular momenta.

</details>

Conclusion: 结果提供了原理性验证，表明基于Grover的量子计算传感可在近期硬件上实现，并能在增加协议复杂度的前提下获得远超盈亏平衡点的计量优势。

Abstract: The combination of quantum sensing with quantum computing to provide an enhancement over conventional quantum sensing has recently emerged as a promising potential application of quantum computing that could give advantages without needing large-scale or fault-tolerant hardware. In this work, we report an experimental demonstration of a recent theoretical proposal to repurpose Grover's search algorithm to improve the ability to detect signals with unknown frequency within a large detection bandwidth. Our experiments were based on a system comprising a single superconducting qubit coupled to a single superconducting cavity, highlighting the modest hardware requirements for realizing the protocol. We found that Grover-based sensing was able to outperform the natural non-Grover baseline for our experimental platform for detection bandwidths $>$10~MHz, with an advantage that empirically grew superlinearly with the bandwidth beyond that break-even point. The use of the Grover-based protocol reduced the amount of signal that needed to be sensed to make an accurate detection decision by more than 10$\times$ for choices of larger detection bandwidth and higher desired detection accuracy. Our results provide a proof-of-principle validation that Grover-based quantum computational sensing can be realized in near-term hardware and provide a metrological advantage well beyond break-even in spite of the additional protocol complexity.

### [87] [Classical Bound on the Fisher Information Rate of a Dephasing-Enhanced Quantum Neuron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35076)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 研究单量子比特在速率ν下的部分重置更新，推导重复投影读出（含测量反作用）的阈值响应带宽与确切局部Fisher信息率；并在固定有限马尔可夫退相位率下，对读出间隔与保留幅度s进行优化。

Abstract: Dephasing can sharpen the activation of a dissipative quantum neuron, but a stationary response does not determine how much input information its output delivers per unit time. For a single qubit with partial-reset updates at rate $ν$, we derive the threshold response bandwidth and the exact local Fisher information rate of the complete record from repeated projective readouts, including their backaction. Near the activation threshold, the rate obeys $\mathcal R\leqν(1-s)(1+s)^2/2\leq16ν/27$, where $s$ is the retained amplitude of each update. For every fixed finite Markovian dephasing rate, optimization over the readout interval and $s$ gives the same supremum, approached at $s=1/3$ with arbitrarily rapid ideal readout. A finite measurement dead time instead selects a finite optimal interval, which we obtain by a dimensionless two-parameter optimization. The ideal bound is saturated by a classical two-state jump process: quantum coherence changes finite-cadence performance but cannot raise the optimized bound. These results separate noise-enhanced activation from the operational information throughput of a quantum neuron and provide a hardware-aware benchmark for finite-time quantum neural processing.

### [88] [From Simple Sources to Quantum Advantage: Homomorphic Polynomial Transduction via Relative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35101)
*Zhong-Xia Shang,Daniel Stilck França*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将解码量子干涉（DQI）及其哈密顿扩展（HDQI）重新表述为经由代数同态的量子态转导，引入相对距离 d_rel 作为新判据，并证明其严格强于普通距离 d_ord，能处理非均匀系数、非对易相互作用并推广至费米子、qudit 与玻色子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI/HDQI 通过傅里叶变换与相干解码制备目标函数低次多项式振幅的量子态，但原始方法依赖复杂且受限的"导频态"制备，且使用普通距离 d_ord，在某些问题（如常数 d_ord 的非线性成对最优多项式相交）中受限。作者希望建立更一般、更模块化的代数框架，放宽对导频态和可交换性的要求。

Method: 将态制备视为通过一元 *-同态 π: 源 C*-代数 → 目标 C*-代数 的量子态转导：先在源哈密顿量 H_A 上高效制备次数为 D 的多项式的算符态，再用算符傅里叶变换与相对解码恢复源算符（而非单个项选择标签），映射到目标哈密顿量 H_B=π(H_A) 的对应多项式态。定义相对距离 d_rel 为源与目标迹首次出现分歧的度数。

Result: 证明 2D<d_rel 等价于保持所有次数至多 D 的多项式之间的内积；且 d_rel≥d_ord。给出 d_ord=O(1) 但 d_rel=Θ(n) 且具有高效解码器的族。框架下 DQI 与 HDQI 成为无关系（relation-free）的特例；支持非均匀系数、非对易相互作用，并扩展至费米子、qudit、玻色子系统，应用于近似优化与 Gibbs 采样。在最优多项式相交的非线性成对变体中，相对解码取得理想量子得分 0.643，优于最佳测试经典启发式的 0.606，差距超过 3 个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过代数同态视角统一并推广了 (H)DQI：相对解码用更模块化的"制备源多项式态"替代复杂导频态，相对距离提供了比普通距离更宽松且可高效实现的条件，并在具体优化任务上展现出超越经典启发式的量子优势潜力。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) and its Hamiltonian extension (HDQI) prepare states whose amplitudes are low-degree polynomials of an objective, using Fourier transforms and coherent decoding. We recast this approach as quantum state transduction through algebra homomorphisms. We transfer an efficiently preparable operator state of a degree-$D$ polynomial in a source Hamiltonian $H_A$ to the corresponding polynomial state of a target Hamiltonian $H_B=π(H_A)$, where $π$ is a unital $*$-homomorphism between the finite-dimensional source and target $C^*$-algebras. The transduction uses operator Fourier transforms and relative decoding to recover source operators rather than individual term-selection labels. The relative distance $d_{\mathrm{rel}}$ is the first degree at which source and target traces disagree. We prove that $2D<d_{\mathrm{rel}}$ is equivalent to preserving all inner products between degree-$D$ polynomials. Moreover, $d_{\mathrm{rel}}\ge d_{\mathrm{ord}}$, where $d_{\mathrm{ord}}$ is the ordinary distance used in (H)DQI, and we give families with $d_{\mathrm{ord}}=O(1)$ but $d_{\mathrm{rel}}=Θ(n)$ and efficient decoders. Our framework replaces the complicated pilot state preparation by the more modular task of preparing a source polynomial state. DQI and HDQI arise as relation-free special cases. The framework accommodates nonuniform coefficients and noncommuting interactions and extends to fermionic, qudit, and bosonic systems, with applications to approximate optimization and Gibbs sampling. As evidence of advantages, for a nonlinear pairwise variant of optimal polynomial intersection where constant $d_{\mathrm{ord}}$ limits DQI-style preparations, relative decoding yields an ideal quantum score of 0.643 versus 0.606 for the best tested classical heuristic, a gap exceeding 3 percentage points.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文将经典LLG方程推导为量子LLG（QLLG）方程在大自旋经典极限下的结果，并对相互作用自旋系统进行了数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该工作给出了耗散自旋动力学的量子到经典描述，为LLG方程提供了微观基础。

Abstract: The Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (LLG) equation provides the fundamental description of dissipative magnetization dynamics. A recently proposed quantum analog, the quantum Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (QLLG) equation, extends this framework to quantum spin systems. However, whether the QLLG equation recovers the LLG equation in the classical large-spin limit for interacting spin systems remains an open question. Here, we demonstrate this connection by deriving the LLG equation as the classical large-spin limit of the QLLG equation. We establish this correspondence analytically and verify it numerically for an interacting spin system. These results provide a quantum-to-classical description of dissipative spin dynamics, offering a microscopic foundation for the LLG equation. From numerical calculations provided here, we also conclude that quantum effects become much less noticeable for systems with larger number of spins in the Hamiltonian.

</details>

Conclusion: 有限区域近似可为量子多体动力学模拟提供可计算且严格的误差保证，并能推广用于评估其他近似方法的可靠性。

Abstract: The exact classical simulation of non-integrable quantum systems rapidly becomes intractable beyond small system sizes, yet its role is central to predicting many-body dynamics. Approximate methods alleviate this cost but typically lack rigorous guarantees on their deviation from the exact result. For the infinite-temperature correlation function, we derive a rigorous error bound for approximating the full dynamics within a computable finite-sized region. Applied to autocorrelations, this finite-region approach is especially accurate across one-dimensional systems and beyond, and can further provide rigorous error bounds for other approximate methods, demonstrated here for matrix-product-operator simulations.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在容错量子计算中，同时具备横向非Clifford门与良好纠错参数的量子码是重要目标，但已有构造往往难以兼顾。本文旨在构造兼具常速率、线性距离与完全可寻址横向T门的显式量子码族。

Method: 将代数几何码与适当的二元嵌入相结合，以获得二元码的广义可除性，进而支撑完全可寻址的横向T门；同时把最小二元嵌入长度问题表述为仿射空间上的最小权问题，并对渐近速率与相对距离界的常数进行数值改进。

Result: 给出了一类显式二元CSS码族，具有常速率与线性距离，并支持完全可寻址横向T门；所构造码可将任意指定的逻辑T门幂次张量积通过物理T门张量积实现，且无需后续纠正。数值上改进了渐近速率与相对距离界的常数。

Conclusion: 代数几何码结合二元嵌入可为量子码提供广义可除性，从而实现完全可寻址横向T门，并在常速率与线性距离之间取得兼顾；最小二元嵌入长度问题可转化为仿射空间最小权问题以优化参数常数。

Abstract: Designing quantum codes with both transversal non-Clifford gates and good error-correcting parameters is an important goal in fault-tolerant quantum computation. Here, we construct an explicit family of binary CSS codes with constant rate and linear distance that admit fully addressable transversal $T$ gates. Specifically, any prescribed tensor product of logical powers of $T$ is implemented by a tensor product of physical powers of $T$, without any subsequent correction. Our construction combines algebraic-geometry codes with suitable binary embedding to obtain generalized divisibility of binary codes. This divisibility then enables fully addressable transversal $T$ gates. Furthermore, we formulate the minimum binary embedding length problem as a minimum-weight problem over an affine space and numerically improve the constants in the asymptotic rate and relative-distance bounds.

</details>

*Mark Goh,Thorge Müller*

TL;DR: 本文证明MF-AOA（量子启发式经典算法）在具有重叠间隙性质（OGP）的问题上无法给出任意近似最优解，因为它等价于受OGP限制的有限内存AMP算法；并通过Max-4-XORSAT的数值模拟显示QAOA能越过OGP屏障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子近似优化算法（QAOA）相对于其量子启发式经典对应物（MF-AOA）是否存在优势，尤其是在具有重叠间隙性质（OGP）的困难问题上——因为AMP等算法已知受OGP限制，作者希望厘清MF-AOA是否同样受限、以及QAOA能否超越这一壁垒。

Method: 将MF-AOA与有限内存近似消息传递（AMP）算法建立联系，从而利用AMP受OGP限制的已知结论来论证MF-AOA的局限性；同时对具有OGP的Max-4-XORSAT实例进行QAOA的数值模拟，并做有限尺寸标度分析，考察QAOA性能与深度依赖。

Result: 理论上证明MF-AOA无法在具有OGP的问题上达到任意近最优解，因为其等价于受OGP限制的有限内存AMP；数值上发现QAOA能越过OGP屏障，且有限尺寸标度分析更支持超多项式而非多项式的深度依赖；此外观察到QAOA性能在OGP屏障附近出现不可导点，最优参数从绝热调度转变为非绝热调度。

Conclusion: 具有OGP的问题是一类QAOA可能整体上优于MF-AOA和AMP算法的实例，说明在此类问题上量子方法相对量子启发式经典方法具有潜在优势。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that the Mean-Field Approximate Optimization Algorithm (MF-AOA), a quantum-inspired classical algorithm of the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA), is unable to give arbitrary near optimal solution for problems exhibiting the Overlap Gap Property (OGP). We show this by relating the MF-AOA to finite-memory Approximate Message Passing (AMP) algorithms, which are known to be limited in performance by the OGP. Thus, our paper argues that problems exhibiting the OGP are one such instance where the QAOA can outperform the MF-AOA and AMP algorithms in general. We supplement this by numerically simulating the performance of the QAOA on instances of Max-$4$-XORSAT that exhibit the OGP and find that the QAOA is able to surpass the barrier, while our finite-size scaling analysis favors a super-polynomial over a polynomial depth dependence. In addition, we observe a non-differentiable point in the performance of the QAOA near by the OGP barrier, where the optimal QAOA parameters change from an adiabatic to a non-adiabatic schedule.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 严格建立了弱相互作用费米子吉布斯态的高斯性（凸混合分解），并给出了多项式时间的经典采样算法。

Conclusion: 弱相互作用费米子系统存在一个可经典高效模拟的参数区间，其吉布斯态具有费米高斯凸混合结构，可被经典多项式时间采样。

Abstract: Identifying regimes in which interacting fermionic systems admit efficient classical simulation is a crucial task in quantum many-body physics. In this work, we rigorously establish the Gaussianity and classical simulability of sufficiently weakly interacting fermions. For the Gibbs state of a local interacting fermionic Hamiltonian $H=H_0+V$ at any finite temperature and in any spatial dimension, where $H_0$ is quadratic and $V$ is the interaction term, we show that there exists a positive interaction strength below which the Gibbs state is a convex mixture of fermionic Gaussian states. Moreover, based on this structure, we construct a polynomial-time classical sampler for the Gibbs state via Metropolis updates on a tree whose leaves are Gaussian states.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IQP QCBM的损失函数可经典高效估计，但推断阶段仍需量子计算机。这使得经典计算机能训练超出可用量子硬件比特数的大型IQP QCBM，因此需要研究如何用量子比特受限的量子计算机对更大的IQP QCBM进行采样。

Conclusion: 该部署协议可使比特受限的量子硬件有效采样大型IQP QCBM，并通过逻辑层噪声注入与重采样缓解误差，在分布相似度和关联结构保持上优于经典基线。

Abstract: Quantum circuit Born machines (QCBMs) built with instantaneous quantum polynomial (IQP) circuits have loss functions that can be efficiently estimated classically, but require quantum computers during inference. Thus, classical computers can train large IQP QCBMs that exceed the qubit numbers of available quantum hardware. This motivates research into how qubit-limited quantum computers can be used to sample from larger IQP QCBMs. Leveraging insights from classical simulation of noisy IQP circuits, we propose a deployment protocol for IQP QCBMs that injects noise at the logical level to probabilistically decompose the original circuit into smaller clusters that can be sampled on qubit-limited quantum processors. We also apply lightweight resampling of known noisy qubits to mitigate the impact of the introduced error. Numerical simulations of our protocol applied to shallow IQP QCBMs and IBM Heron experiments deploying a 316-qubit IQP circuit onto 156-qubit hardware show that mitigation improves the similarity of the empirical distribution to the target distribution. The proposed deployment protocol also crucially preserves correlation structures more accurately than a classical baseline that has accurate single-qubit marginals.

</details>


### [95] [Positronium Laser Deceleration, Cooling and Trapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35202)
*Barna Mendei,Helmut Ritsch*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正电子素（Ps）基态寿命极短且反冲位移与线宽之比很高，给激光减速、冷却与囚禁带来新的时序和激光功率限制；而制备超冷、致密 Ps 系综是超辐射激光与玻色–爱因斯坦凝聚等研究的基础，因此需评估实际可行性与所需参数。

Method: 扩展数值研究：考虑源于二氧化硅微通道靶、由 Surko 陷阱正电子束产生的快速基态正电子素（o-Ps）束；在 $1^3\mathrm{S}$–$2^3\mathrm{P}$ 跃迁上进行标准多普勒冷却几何的辐射压力减速、冷却与光学囚禁；纳入完整的塞曼态流形，并额外在 $2^3\mathrm{P}$–$3^3\mathrm{D}$ 跃迁上施加横向激光。

Result: 数值结果确认在标准多普勒冷却几何下同时减速、冷却并光学囚禁 Ps 原子具有良好的可行性；引入 $2^3\mathrm{P}$–$3^3\mathrm{D}$ 横向激光并考虑完整塞曼子能级后，时序和最终温度得到改善，可实现末端光学囚禁；所需激光功率与几何参数处于现有技术范围内，即约 $\mathrm{mJ}$ 能量、约 $100\ \mathrm{ns}$ 脉宽的脉冲。

Conclusion: 在大多数原子尚未湮灭的时间窗口内实现有效减速与囚禁在当前技术条件下可行；未来通过光学腔或空芯光纤实现集体增强冷却与囚禁，有望快速制备超冷 Ps 体系，作为超辐射激光和玻色–爱因斯坦凝聚的基础。

Abstract: We numerically study laser deceleration, cooling and trapping of host fast ortho-positronium (Ps) bunches as produced in silica microchannel targets using positron bunches from a Surko trap. While Doppler laser cooling routinely allows to cool neutral atom species below milli-Kelvin kinetic temperatures, the fast ground state annihilation and the high recoil shift to line-width ratio in Ps pose new challenging limitations on timing and the required laser power. Earlier theoretical work and first experiments already exhibit viable prospects for radiation pressure cooling to create unprecedented cold and dense Ps ensembles. Our extended numerical studies confirm very good prospects to simultaneously decelerate, cool and optically trap Ps atoms in a standard Doppler cooling geometry on the $1^3\mathrm{S}$--$2^3\mathrm{P}$ transition. Including the full Zeeman state manifolds and adding additional transverse lasers on the $2^3\mathrm{P}$--$3^3\mathrm{D}$ transition improves timing and final temperature to allow for optical trapping at the end. The required laser powers and geometry parameters to implement effective slowing and trapping within a time frame, where the majority of atoms is not annihilated, is in reach of current technology of $\mathrm{mJ}$ energy pulses of approximately $100\ \mathrm{ns}$ duration. Future extensions to collectively enhance cooling and trapping in optical cavities or hollow-core fibres should finally allow fast preparation of ultra-cold Ps systems as future basis of superradiant lasing and Bose--Einstein condensates.

</details>


### [96] [A technological capability scale for civilizations from the Kardashev and Barrow scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35237)
*Volkan Gurses*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出以"每秒可执行的控制操作数"衡量文明技术水平的新标度，将Kardashev指数K与能效指数B相加得Ξ=K+B；1950年以来每次逻辑操作能耗下降11.7个数量级，人类当前K=0.73、B=-0.78（CMOS一次能源计），终极技术Ξ=7.58。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Kardashev标度只按能量功率对文明分级，Barrow标度只按信息容量（比特数）分级，二者都未直接刻画"能对物质施加多少可控操作"。作者希望建立一个统一的技术能力标度，把能量、信息与控制操作三者纳入同一框架，从而既能给出给定热力学条件下的能力上限与下限，也能定位人类当前水平与终极物理极限。

Method: 以constructor theory为基础，将"控制操作"定义为把物质的一个二值自由度设为指定值（不论其先前状态），并划分为信息介质上的构造及其特例——处理、存储、传输。定义指数Ξ=K+B：K为Kardashev指数（功率），B为每次操作能耗相对微波背景温度热下限的效率指数。用Kardashev标度给出向当前天空辐射散热、重置热随机比特的上限，用Barrow标度给出下限（即每一层级排热温度下的热下限）。据此对人类技术水平与终极技术进行量化比较。

Result: 1950年以来，每次逻辑操作的能耗下降了11.7个数量级，其中约5个数量级来自器件微型化。人类目前以CMOS逻辑门计算，K=0.73，按一次能源计B=-0.78。所描述的终极技术可达Ξ=7.58，其耗散功率为推测的最大功率c^5/4G，散热对象为de Sitter空间中接近最大质量的非带电、非旋转黑洞。

Conclusion: 该标度将能量功率与每次操作能效耦合为单一指标Ξ，为文明技术水平提供了可跨尺度比较的量化框架，并同时给出能力上限（Kardashev）与热力学下限（Barrow）。它表明人类在能效维度仍有巨大提升空间，而物理定律所允许的终极技术极限约为Ξ=7.58。

Abstract: We introduce a technological capability scale that ranks civilizations by the control operations they can perform per second. A control operation is defined as setting one binary degree of freedom of matter to a specified value, whatever its previous state. Building on constructor theory, we sort them into construction and its special cases on information media, namely processing, storage and transfer. The index $Ξ= K + B$ adds to the Kardashev index $K$ an efficiency index $B$ of energy per operation, measured from the thermal floor at the microwave background temperature. The Kardashev scale gives the ceiling for resets of thermally random bits with heat radiated into the present sky, and the Barrow scale gives the floor, which for such a reset is the thermal floor at the heat-rejection temperature at every level. Since 1950 the energy per logic operation has fallen 11.7 orders of magnitude, around 5 through miniaturization. Humanity, at $K = 0.73$, computes with CMOS logic gates at $B = -0.78$ in primary energy. We describe the ultimate technology, at $Ξ= 7.58$, which would dissipate the conjectured largest power $c^5/4G$ into an uncharged, non-rotating black hole near its largest mass in de Sitter space.

</details>


### [97] [Computational Complexity of Clifford Template Compilation: Are Quantum Computers Useful for Compiling Quantum Circuits?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35239)
*Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Clifford模板编译问题：给定可重复Clifford操作的有序族，选择各操作重复次数以实现目标Pauli算子变换。该逆向问题呈现丰富的复杂性谱：对交换模板且整数指数无约束，可行性属于NP∩BQP并可构造性求解；限制指数为{0,1}时，即使仅含CNOT与X型Pauli也NP完全；对交换自逆Clifford作用，二元可行性与求一解多项式可解，但总重复次数受限时仍NP完全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于量子纠错中在物理或容错约束下仅用允许的Clifford操作搜索逻辑操作的需求。尽管正向Clifford动力学可高效经典模拟，但其逆向问题存在尖锐的复杂性转变，值得系统刻画其复杂性边界。

Method: 将Clifford模板形式化为有限有序的可重复Clifford操作族，实例由各操作的重复次数指定。分别考察：(1) 交换模板、指数为无约束整数；(2) 一般情形下限制指数为{0,1}；(3) 交换自逆Clifford作用；(4) 对总重复次数施加界的变体。通过构造性量子算法、有限域离散对数归约及NP完全性归约进行分析。

Result: 交换模板且整数指数无约束时，可行性属于NP∩BQP，构造性量子算法可返回一个特解及完整指数关系格；即使k=1，恢复重复次数已包含F_{2^r}^×上的有限域离散对数。一般情形下限制指数为{0,1}会破坏Abel群闭包，使变k时可行性NP完全，即使仅含精确交换的CNOT操作与X型Pauli。对交换自逆Clifford作用，二元可行性与恢复一个解均经典多项式时间可解，但对总重复次数加界后仍NP完全，即使仅含CNOT操作。

Conclusion: Clifford模板编译问题涵盖经典可解情形、具有量子多项式时间算法的问题以及NP完全变体，揭示了一个层次丰富的复杂性图景。

Abstract: A Clifford template is a finite ordered family of repeatable Clifford operations, and an instantiation specifies how many times each operation is applied. The Clifford template compilation problem asks how to choose these repetition numbers so that the template realizes a target transformation of Pauli operators. This problem arises, for example, when searching for logical operations in quantum error correction using only Clifford operations permitted by physical or fault-tolerance constraints. Although forward Clifford dynamics is efficiently classically simulable, this inverse problem has sharp complexity transitions. For commuting templates with unrestricted integer exponents, feasibility lies in $\mathrm{NP}\cap\mathrm{BQP}$ and a constructive quantum algorithm returns a particular solution together with the full exponent-relation lattice; already at $k=1$, recovering the repetition number contains finite-field discrete logarithm over $\mathbb{F}_{2^r}^{\times}$. In general, restricting every exponent to $\{0,1\}$ removes the Abelian-group closure and makes feasibility NP-complete for variable $k$, even for exactly commuting CNOT-only operations and X-type Paulis. For commuting self-inverse Clifford actions, both binary feasibility and recovery of one solution are classically polynomial-time solvable, but imposing a bound on the total repetition count is NP-complete, even for CNOT-only operations. These results reveal a rich complexity landscape within Clifford template compilation, spanning classically tractable cases, problems admitting quantum polynomial-time algorithms, and NP-complete variants.

</details>


### [98] [An Exact Polynomial Task-Risk Bridge for Time-Multiplexed Photonic Quantum Reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35243)
*Yuqi Zhang,Tianyu Zhou,Yilun Jiang,Tian Chen,Hao Tang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对TDM光子储备池"仅靠少数数据集评分、依靠穷举仿真设计"的缺陷，提出一套从任务到预测风险的精确理论桥接方法，包含任务特征、编码与读出三个步骤，并用七个数据集验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TDM光子储备池通常仅用少数数据集上的得分来评判，难以反映芯片整体性能；其电路设计依赖枚举候选结构并逐一双重仿真，效率低且无法保证找到良好结构。作者希望以精确理论替代试错仿真来刻画并剪枝候选架构空间。

Method: 针对时间展开的无源线性网络（含数据调制门/源与岭回归读出），分三步推导任务到预测风险的精确关系：(i)任务特征：有限次采样风险仅通过训练窗口的有限统计量及其与目标的相关性依赖时间序列，由编码与光路选择；(ii)编码：每个输出矩是数据调制位点特征的多项式，其阶数与支撑由光路元组界定；门编码读取特征函数并跨时间延迟产生相互作用，压缩编码读取矩生成函数，位移编码读取低阶矩，且在正交接收下对任意拓扑分别仅给出加性与线性模型；(iii)读出：对零差、外差与光子数接收机风险可精确闭合，其对采样次数的依赖为信噪比模式上的显式求和，阈值点击允许带认证误差的有限字典近似。互不共享光的部分具有独立态，接收机仅通过连接它们的测量事件进行组合。

Result: 所提出的每一条精确陈述均在七个数据集上被验证至机器精度；理论给出了由精确陈述而非试错仿真剪枝的TDM架构候选空间。

Conclusion: 三步框架构建了有用的TDM候选架构空间，可通过精确陈述进行剪枝，未来可用于根据目标需求指导TDM芯片的选型。

Abstract: Time-multiplexed (TDM) photonic reservoirs are usually judged by their scores on a few datasets, which say little about a chip's overall performance, and designed by enumerating candidate circuits and simulating each one, which is inefficient and gives no guarantee of finding a good structure. For a time-unrolled passive linear network with data-modulated gates or sources and a ridge readout, we derive an exact bridge from the task to the prediction risk in three steps. (i) Task features: the finite-shot risk depends on the time series only through finitely many statistics of its training windows and their correlations with the target, selected by the encoding and the optical paths. (ii) Encoding: every output moment is a polynomial in the features of the data-modulated sites, with degree and support bounded by tuples of optical paths. Gate encoding reads the characteristic function and creates interactions across time lags; squeezing encoding reads the moment-generating function and displacement encoding low-order moments, and with quadrature receivers they yield only additive and linear models, respectively, for every topology. (iii) Readout: for homodyne, heterodyne and photon-number receivers the risk closes exactly, and its dependence on the number of shots is an explicit sum over signal-to-noise modes; threshold clicks admit finite-dictionary approximations with certified error. Parts of the network that share no light carry independent states, which a receiver combines only through measurement events that join them. Checks on seven datasets confirm every exact statement to machine precision. The three steps thus yield a useful space of candidate TDM architectures, pruned by exact statements rather than by trial simulation, which can in future guide the selection of TDM chips for target requirements.

</details>


### [99] [Gauge freedom and efficient algorithms for Lindbladian learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35265)
*Steven T Flammia,Savar D Sinha,Yu Tong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在SPAM噪声下学习局部Lindbladian的问题，刻画了由于规范变换导致的本质不可学习障碍，并为所有普适规范不变量分量提供了SPAM鲁棒的、仅用受信单比特操作的scalable学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期虽有可扩展的Lindbladian学习算法，但都依赖理想化的访问假设。在实际实验中，状态制备与测量（SPAM）噪声会使不同的Lindbladian在实验上无法区分，从而导致Lindbladian的某些部分从根本上不可学习。因此需要刻画这种内在障碍，并寻找在SPAM下仍可学习的分量。

Method: 针对有界度局部Lindbladian：(1) 识别出一族与受信单比特控制对易的保局域性规范变换，并据此对Lindbladian分量进行分类；(2) 对一般规范相关分量，构造即使满足完全正性约束仍具有Ω(1)不确定性的Lindbladian作为障碍实例；(3) 为每个普适规范不变量分量设计SPAM鲁棒学习算法，仅使用受信单比特操作，无需辅助比特和纠缠控制。

Result: 对一般规范相关分量证明了与系统规模无关的Ω(1)不确定性（即便施加完全正性）。所提算法仅用受信单比特操作，不需要辅助比特或纠缠控制，在常数局域性和度数下，实验次数与总演化时间为Õ(ε^{-2}log(N/δ))。当N增大时允许全局SPAM任意偏离理想情况，仅需非消失的局部可见度。此外，在物理正性约束下给出了额外哈密顿量系数的边界诱导规范不变的充分条件。

Conclusion: SPAM噪声通过规范变换使部分Lindbladian分量实验上不可区分，构成根本性可学习性限制；但所有普适规范不变量分量都可通过SPAM鲁棒算法高效学习。更广泛地，所提出的规范感知框架与SPAM消除技术为开放量子系统的可扩展刻画提供了实用工具。在一般情况下学习额外的规范相关参数仍是公开问题。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning a local Lindbladian in the presence of state-preparation-and-measurement (SPAM) noise. Although recent work has developed scalable learning algorithms under idealized access assumptions, SPAM can make distinct Lindbladians experimentally indistinguishable, thus making part of the Lindbladian fundamentally unlearnable. We give a sharp characterization of this obstruction for bounded-degree local Lindbladians. We identify a family of locality-preserving gauge transformations that commutes with trusted single-qubit control, and use it to classify Lindbladian components. For the generically gauge dependent components, we construct Lindbladians to show that they can have $Ω(1)$ uncertainty independent of system size, even after imposing complete positivity.
  We complement this characterization with SPAM-robust algorithms for learning every universally gauge-invariant component. Our algorithms use only trusted single-qubit operations, require neither ancillas nor entangling control, and use $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\left(ε^{-2}\log(N/δ)\right)$ experiments and total evolution time for constant locality and degree. Our guarantees allow global SPAM to become arbitrarily far from ideal as $N$ grows, requiring only nonvanishing local visibility. We also establish sufficient conditions for boundary-induced gauge invariance of additional Hamiltonian coefficients under physical positivity constraints, though learning these additional parameters in general remains an open problem. More broadly, our gauge-aware framework and SPAM-cancellation techniques offer a practical toolkit for the scalable characterization of open quantum systems.

</details>


### [100] [Generalized LIMDDs: Succinctness and Canonicity for Decision Diagrams Modulo a Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35271)
*Arend-Jan Quist,Alexis de Colnet,Thomas Reps,Alfons Laarman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究将ROBDD的Myhill–Nerode商按群作用粗化所得的决策图（LIMDD推广），刻画了由C^{≤k}R_q生成的两参数群族的简洁性阶，给出五规则归约系统与多项式时间规范形算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROBDD是固定长度字上函数的最小自动机（残差+Myhill–Nerode商）。若用群作用粗化该商（按G-轨道合并节点并在边上记录群元素），就得到Pauli-LIMDD等结构。作者希望把这种构造推广到更一般的群族，弄清其简洁性收益、查询/变换的可处理性以及是否存在唯一的规范形。

Method: 从残差上的群作用出发构造粗化决策图；取由C^{≤k}R_q（阶为q的相位旋转，最多k个控制量子比特）生成的两参数群族，分带/不带比特翻转X两种情形；以超图态为分离对象；设计五条归约规则并分析其终止性与合流性。

Result: 该族相对Pauli-LIMDD有指数级简洁性提升，且完整确定了该族的简洁性阶；超图态总可被族中某个成员高效表示；八个变换与五个查询的可处理性在整个族内不变，且与Pauli-LIMDD行为相同；五规则归约系统对族中每个成员给出唯一正规形，且可在LIMDD规模的多项式时间内计算。

Conclusion: 在(反)对角群之内粗化时规范形高效且规范；但超出该范围后，最小尺寸正规形的计算变为非局部的，可能需要重建整个图，说明简洁性收益与规范化代价之间存在本质张力。

Abstract: A reduced ordered decision diagram is the minimal automaton of a function on words of fixed length: its nodes are the residuals, and reduction is the Myhill--Nerode quotient. We study what happens when that quotient is coarsened by a group. Fix a group $G$ acting on residuals, merge two nodes when their residuals lie in one $G$-orbit, and record the group element on the edge. For functions $\{0,1\}^n\to\mathbb{C}$ and $G$ the Pauli group this is the Local Invertible Map Decision Diagram. We take $G$ from the two-parameter family generated by $C^{\leq k}R_q$, the phase rotation of order $q$ with up to $k$ control qubits, with and without the bit flip $X$. We show that this gives exponential succinctness improvements compared to Pauli-LIMDD, and we determine the succinctness order of the family completely. The separating objects are hypergraph states, which can always be efficiently represented by some member of the family. We settle the tractability of five queries and eight transformations, which is invariant across the family, and shows the same behavior as Pauli-LIMDD. We give a five-rule reduction system whose normal forms are unique for every member of the family, and we show that this canonical form is computable in polynomial time in the size of the LIMDD. We show that, when coarsening beyond the (anti-)diagonal groups, the calculation of a minimal sized normal form turns out to be non-local and it might to rebuild the whole diagram.

</details>


### [101] [Circuit-level benchmarks of GKP-concatenated qLDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35282)
*Yuan Yao,Ruipeng Xing,Jianshuo Gao,Xiao Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在统一仿真框架下比较BB码与tricycle码作为GKP码外层码的性能，涵盖码容量噪声、重复噪声伴随测量与调度解析的位移传播三种噪声模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展的容错GKP量子纠错需要有限速率的外层码，且需在真实电路噪声下利用GKP的模拟信息。然而，qLDPC外层码尚未作为电路级GKP级联候选被系统比较。

Method: 建立统一仿真框架，从码容量噪声逐步推进到带噪重复综合征提取及调度解析的位移传播；对BB码和tricycle码比较硬判决与模拟信息辅助的BP-OSD译码。

Result: 三种噪声模型下，模拟信息辅助BP-OSD的有限尺寸交叉阈值估计均高于硬判决译码。全电路模型中，BB序列阈值为σ_th≈0.212（约10.46 dB压缩），tricycle序列为0.142（约13.94 dB压缩）。

Conclusion: 模拟GKP信息在电路级噪声下仍能提升译码性能；在本仿真假设下，所选BB序列的有限尺寸交叉估计高于所选tricycle序列。

Abstract: Scalable fault-tolerant quantum error correction based on Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes requires finite-rate outer codes that can exploit analog GKP information under realistic circuit noise. However, quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) outer codes have not been systematically compared as candidates for circuit-level GKP concatenation. Here, we benchmark BB and tricycle codes using a unified simulation framework that progresses from code-capacity noise to repeated noisy syndrome extraction and schedule-resolved displacement propagation. Across all three noise models, analog-informed belief-propagation with ordered-statistics decoding (BP-OSD) yields higher finite-size crossing estimates than hard-decision decoding. In the full circuit model, the analog-informed crossings are \(σ_{\mathrm{th}}\simeq0.212\) for the selected BB sequence and \(0.142\) for the tricycle sequence, corresponding to squeezing requirements of approximately \(10.46\,\mathrm{dB}\) and \(13.94\,\mathrm{dB}\), respectively. These results show that analog GKP information continues to improve decoding under circuit-level noise. Under the common simulation assumptions used here, the selected BB sequence also yields a higher finite-size crossing estimate than the selected tricycle sequence.

</details>


### [102] [Distributional Variants of the Aaronson-Ambainis Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35327)
*Uma Girish,Kunal Mittal,Barak Nehoran,Ran Raz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：在偏置乘积分布和固定汉明重量切片分布下，量子查询算法相对经典查询算法的多项式模拟问题，均等价于均匀分布下的原始猜想；并证明对应的Aaronson-Ambainis多项式影响力变体也与原猜想等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子复杂度理论中长期存在一个猜想：在均匀输入分布下，量子查询算法可被经典查询算法以多项式查询开销模拟。该猜想关乎指数级量子优势是否必须依赖额外结构。作者希望理解该猜想是否依赖于均匀分布这一特定选择，即其他自然输入分布下是否有类似结论。

Method: 作者研究两类自然分布的类比：(1) 偏置乘积分布μ_p，输入比特为独立Bernoulli变量，偏置固定为p；(2) 切片分布ν_p，在汉明重量为⌊pn⌋的字符串集合上均匀分布。作者分别在这些分布下定义量子查询算法的多项式开销经典模拟问题，并证明其与原均匀分布猜想等价。此外，作者在布尔超立方体上有界低次多项式框架下，针对偏置乘积分布和切片分布表述Aaronson-Ambainis猜想的类比，并证明这些变体与原Aaronson-Ambainis猜想等价。

Result: 对于每个固定p∈(0,1)，μ_p分布下量子查询算法可多项式开销经典模拟当且仅当均匀分布下同样成立；ν_p分布与均匀分布之间也成立类似的等价结论。多项式影响力方面，偏置乘积分布和切片分布下的Aaronson-Ambainis型变体均与原Aaronson-Ambainis猜想等价。

Conclusion: 该猜想的核心难点并不依赖于均匀分布这一特殊假设：在偏置乘积分布和固定汉明重量切片分布下，相应的量子模拟问题与多项式影响力问题都与均匀分布情形等价。这表明原猜想具有更强的分布鲁棒性，也为研究该猜想提供了等价的替代形式。

Abstract: A longstanding conjecture in quantum complexity theory asserts that, under the uniform input distribution, quantum query algorithms can be polynomially simulated by classical query algorithms. More precisely, the acceptance probability of any quantum query algorithm can be approximated, on average over uniformly random inputs, by a classical query algorithm, with only polynomial query overhead. The conjecture is central to understanding whether exponential quantum advantages for decision problems necessarily rely on additional structure.
  We study analogues of this conjecture under other natural input distributions and prove that they are all equivalent to the original uniform-distribution conjecture. We first consider the product distribution $μ_p$, where the input bits are independent Bernoulli variables with fixed bias $p$. We show that for every fixed $p \in (0, 1)$, quantum query algorithms under the $μ_p$ distribution admit polynomial-overhead classical simulations if and only if the same holds under the uniform distribution. Second, we consider the distribution $ν_p$ that is uniform over the slice of strings with Hamming weight $\lfloor pn \rfloor$ and prove a similar equivalence for the $ν_p$ distribution and the uniform distribution.
  The Aaronson-Ambainis conjecture is a stronger statement that implies the above-mentioned conjecture and is formulated in terms of bounded low-degree polynomials on the Boolean hypercube. It asserts that under the uniform distribution, any such polynomial with nonnegligible variance must have an influential variable. We formulate analogues of this conjecture, where the underlying distribution is a biased product distribution or a uniform distribution over a slice, and prove that all these variants are equivalent to the original Aaronson-Ambainis conjecture.

</details>


### [103] [Heuristic lower bounds on real Grothendieck constants of finite and infinite order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35399)
*Erika Bene,Tamás Vértesi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用旋转不变三次核，构造候选下界并给出有限维矩阵，条件性改进实Grothendieck常数及其极限的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实Grothendieck常数K_G(d)及其无穷维极限K_G的精确值未知，已有严格下界K_G≥6π/11≈1.713596仍可能与真值有差距，需要新的构造与证据来逼近真值并给出改进下界。

Method: 采用旋转不变三次核进行连续构造，识别半球形二元策略在三次边界形变下失稳的显式阈值，并用数值证据支持该阈值处半球策略全局最优的猜想；同时从d≤24的单位向量构造有限对称系数矩阵，并用大规模see-saw优化进行验证。

Result: 猜想若成立可推出K_G≥9π/16≈1.767146，优于严格下界6π/11≈1.713596；see-saw优化支持连续预测，并在4至24维给出改进的K_G(d)候选下界（条件性）；部分矩阵作为双分关联贝尔泛函时，若相应二元上界成立，可作为维度见证，证明每方局域Hilbert空间维度至少为5。

Conclusion: 通过三次核构造与数值优化，给出改进实Grothendieck常数下界的候选方案及有限维维度见证，但其最优性与下界改进均依赖于关于二元最优的猜想成立。

Abstract: We propose candidate lower bounds on the real Grothendieck constants $K_{\mathrm G}(d)$ and their infinite-dimensional limit $K_{\mathrm G}$ using rotationally invariant cubic kernels. For the continuous construction, we identify an explicit threshold above which hemispherical binary strategies are unstable under cubic boundary deformations. Supported by numerical evidence, we conjecture that hemispherical strategies are globally optimal at this threshold. This conjecture would imply $K_{\mathrm G}\geq9π/16\simeq1.767146$, improving the rigorous lower bound $6π/11\simeq1.713596$. We also construct finite symmetric coefficient matrices from unit vectors in dimensions up to $24$. Large-scale see-saw optimization supports the continuous predictions and suggests improved lower bounds on $K_{\mathrm G}(d)$ in the studied dimensions from $4$ to $24$, conditional on the conjectured binary optima being exact. Selected matrices also yield finite-setting candidate dimension witnesses when interpreted as bipartite correlation Bell functionals. If the corresponding binary upper bounds hold, these witnesses would certify a local Hilbert-space dimension of at least five for each party.

</details>


### [104] [Fixed-Time Gaussian State Transfer via Collective Dissipation in a Fully Static Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35446)
*Austen Couvertier,Ting Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出一种仅依赖集体耗散的固定时间高斯态传输机制，无需相干输运与时变控制，转移时间解析为 $t^{*}=\pi/g_a$，并系统分析鲁棒性与非马尔可夫效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统连续变量通信通常假设需要相干输运或主动控制才能实现态传输。本文旨在挑战这一假设，探索能否仅靠集体耗散（与环境耦合）实现固定时间的高斯态传输，而无需时间依赖控制。

Method: 建立完全静态、纯耗散的集体环境耦合模型，动力学在明-暗（bright–dark）子空间中闭合分解，得到解析转移时间 $t^{*}=\pi/g_a$ 与实数干涉因子以消除动力学相位积累；通过系综分析考察幅度不对称与相位失谐的影响；引入有限 Ornstein–Uhlenbeck 记忆并采用零阶 O-算子展开纳入非马尔可夫效应，并分析快记忆极限。

Result: 获得解析转移时间 $t^{*}=\pi/g_a$；实数干涉因子消除动力学相位积累，相位敏感性仅来自高斯保真度度量，构成固定时间高斯信道。系综分析显示信道对幅度级不对称鲁棒（表现为均匀速率修饰），而相位级系统失谐通过动力学相位缠绕破坏固定时间机制并产生复苏行为。有限 OU 记忆仅导致速率与相位重整化，不引入新动力学通道；快记忆区环境关联被快速抑制，信道对环境失谐不敏感并恢复马尔可夫极限。

Conclusion: 集体耗散本身即可支持固定时间高斯态传输，无需相干输运或主动控制，挑战了连续变量通信必须依赖相干输运或主动控制的既有假设。

Abstract: We establish a fully static, dissipation-only primitive for Gaussian state transfer in which collective coupling to a shared environment fixes a single transfer time without coherent transport or time-dependent control. The dynamics close in a bright--dark decomposition, yielding an analytic transfer time $t^{*} = π/ g_{a}$ and a real interference factor that eliminates dynamical phase accumulation, so that all phase sensitivity arises solely from the Gaussian fidelity metric. This defines a fixed-time Gaussian communication channel in which dissipation mediates information transfer without active control. Ensemble analysis shows that channel performance remains robust to amplitude-level asymmetries, which act as uniform-rate dressing, whereas phase-level system detuning disrupts the fixed-time mechanism via dynamical phase winding and produces revival behavior. Incorporating finite Ornstein--Uhlenbeck memory via a zeroth-order O-operator expansion yields only rate and phase renormalization without introducing new dynamical pathways. In the fast-memory regime, environmental correlations are rapidly suppressed, rendering the channel insensitive to environmental detuning and restoring the Markovian limit. These results show that collective dissipation alone is sufficient to support fixed-time Gaussian state transfer, challenging the assumption that coherent transport or active control are required for continuous-variable communication.

</details>


### [105] [Efficient classical algorithm for estimating linear statistics of Boson Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35447)
*Benoit Seron,Hugo Thomas,Eduardo Araujo,Alex Arkhipov,Changhun Oh,Leonardo Novo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了玻色采样输出分布中基于光子占据数线性函数的"线性统计量"（linear statistics）的粗粒化问题，给出了在加性误差下高效近似此类统计量的经典算法，统一了分子振动光谱模拟与探测器分箱等已知结果，并讨论了其对某个基于玻色采样的单向函数的安全性冲击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色采样是展示量子计算优越性的重要候选方案，但大规模玻色采样器能否用于有用计算仍不明确。利用玻色采样估计物理观测量时，由于输出空间指数级大且分布具有反集中性质，必须对输出分布进行粗粒化；因此需要理解这类粗粒化任务的计算复杂度。

Method: 作者针对基于输出光子占据数线性函数的特定粗粒化方式（即线性统计量）展开复杂度分析，针对Fock态、压缩态等不同输入态，构造了在加性误差范围内高效近似线性统计量的经典算法。

Result: 该经典算法能够在加性误差内高效近似多类输入态（Fock态、压缩态）下的线性统计量，从而在同一框架下统一了近期关于分子振动光谱的量子启发式经典模拟算法以及基于探测器分箱的粗粒化分布经典近似结果；同时该算法可用于经典地评估某个基于玻色采样的单向函数，而其他密码学应用提案则不受此经典模拟技术影响。

Conclusion: 线性统计量的玻色采样粗粒化在加性误差下可被经典高效模拟。非线性统计量的经典可模拟性仍为开放问题，作者将其与"含单层相互作用的线性光学电路跃迁振幅计算"问题相联系。

Abstract: Boson Sampling is a prominent candidate for the demonstration of quantum computational advantage but it remains unclear whether a large-scale boson sampler can find useful computational applications. The challenge is that to use a boson sampler to estimate, for example, a physical observable, it is necessary to coarse grain the outcome distribution, owing to the exponential size of the outcome space and the anti-concentration properties of the Boson Sampling distribution. In this work, we analyse the complexity of a specific type of coarse-graining of Boson Sampling distributions based on linear functions of the output photon occupation numbers, which we refer to as linear statistics. We present an efficient classical algorithm for approximating linear statistics of boson samplers within additive error for different kinds of input states, such as Fock states and squeezed states. This allows us to unify in the same framework recent results on efficient quantum-inspired classical algorithms for simulating molecular vibronic spectra, or efficient classical approximations of coarse-grained distributions based on detector binning. Additionally, we show how our algorithm can be used to classically evaluate a proposed one-way function based on Boson Sampling, while other proposals for cryptographic applications escape our classical simulation techniques. We leave open the question of classical simulability of non-linear statistics and connect it to the problem of computing transition amplitudes of linear-optical circuits with one layer of interactions.

</details>


### [106] [Improving the Stability of the Hierarchical Equations of Motion for Open Quantum Systems with Strong Coupling to Structured Bosonic Baths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35484)
*Salvatore Gatto,Samuel L. Rudge,Bokang Hou,Eran Rabani,Michael Thoss*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对HEOM在玻色子环境中因有限截断导致的数值不稳定性，利用辅助Fock空间图像识别失衡的层级升阶项，并通过非幺正相似变换将其转为升降平衡组合，从而抑制瞬态放大。以Brownian振子谱密度和结构谱密度验证，变换后的HEOM可模拟比常规方法大得多的系统-浴耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HEOM是模拟开放量子系统动力学最强大的方法之一，在许多参数区间和物理场景中稳定高效，但玻色子环境中不可避免的有限层级截断会引发基本数值不稳定性，亟需分析并解决该问题。

Method: 采用辅助Fock空间图像描述层级结构，识别导致强瞬态放大的失衡层级升阶项（与非正规代数结构相关）；再施加非幺正相似变换，将失衡项转化为层级升阶与降阶项的平衡组合，以抑制其数值影响。

Result: 在简单Brownian振子谱密度的自旋-玻色模型中验证了方法有效性，并进一步推广至结构谱密度情形；两种情况下，变换后的HEOM均能实现远大于常规方法的系统-浴耦合模拟范围。

Conclusion: 通过辅助Fock空间分析与非幺正相似变换可有效抑制HEOM的截断数值不稳定性，显著扩展玻色子环境下可稳定模拟的系统-浴耦合强度。

Abstract: The hierarchical equations of motion (HEOM) approach is one of the most powerful methods avail- able to simulate the dynamics of open quantum systems. Although the HEOM approach has been shown to be stable and efficient in many parameter regimes and physical scenarios, it is also well known that the unavoidable finite truncation of the hierarchy can lead to fundamental numeri- cal instabilities for bosonic environments. In this work, we apply recently developed techniques to analyze these issues. We use an auxiliary Fock-space picture of the hierarchy to first identify the unbalanced hierarchy-raising terms responsible for the strong transient amplification associated with the non-normal algebraic structure of the HEOM generator. We then apply a non-unitary similarity transformation that converts these terms into balanced combinations of hierarchy-raising and hierarchy-lowering terms, thereby suppressing their numerical impact. We first demonstrate the efficacy of this approach via a spin-boson model with a simple Brownian oscillator spectral den- sity and subsequently extend the analysis to a structured spectral density, showing for both cases that the transformed HEOM approach allows simulations of much larger system-bath coupling than would otherwise be possible.

</details>


### [107] [Temporal trade-offs in high-dimensional entanglement: a comprehensive noise model for optimal time-bin QKD protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35488)
*Alexandra E. Bergmayr-Mann,Gláucia Murta,Marcus Huber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对高维时间bin纠缠在QKD中是否优于低维编码的问题，建立灵活噪声模型，在单Franson干涉仪与渐近密钥率/共享纠缠指标下给出定量判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维时间bin纠缠易实现、鲁棒且可能提供优于简单qubit协议的密钥率，但时间编码与有限时钟分辨率带来权衡：应发送更多低维编码光子，还是少量高维光子？该问题依赖协议整体背景（产生率、损耗、暗计数、时间抖动等），难以回答，本文旨在系统解决该权衡问题。

Method: 针对单个Franson基干涉仪，以渐近密钥率和共享纠缠为主要品质因数；构建包含所有相关参数的灵活噪声模型（涵盖产生率、损耗、暗计数、时间抖动等），并据此分析不同编码维度下的性能对比。

Result: 分析表明确实存在若干区域，当光子对到达率受限（例如由损耗或泵浦功率限制导致）时，高维编码性能仍优于任何基于qubit的版本。

Conclusion: 高维时间bin编码并非在所有条件下占优；其优势依赖于系统具体参数，尤其是在对到达率受限的场景中可超越qubit协议，为QKD编码维度选择提供依据。

Abstract: High-dimensional time-bin entanglement is known for having high potential for quantum key distribution (QKD) applications, being easily implementable, robust and may offer better keyrates than simple qubit protocols. The temporal encoding, combined with limited clock resolution, however, implies a trade-off: Is it better to send more low-dimensionally encoded photons or few high-dimensional ones? Answering this question presents a hard challenge as it depends on the entire context of the protocol, including the production rates, losses, dark counts, timing jitters and many more. We answer this question for single Franson based interferometers and asymptotic key rate and shared entanglement as main figures of merit. We present a flexible noise model incorporating all relevant parameters and find that there are indeed regions where high-dimensional encoding still outperforms any qubit based version when the pair arrival rate is limited, for instance through loss or limited pump power.

</details>


### [108] [Fourier-Geometric Circuit Design for Gate and Entanglement Placement in Quantum Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35489)
*Seungcheol Oh,Chaemoon Im,Daeyeun Kim,Soohyun Park,Vaneet Aggarwal,Mohsen Heidari,Joongheon Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于编码生成元伴随之作用的显式电路设计准则，通过将算子空间分解为按频率索引的二维不变平面，确定使目标傅里叶系数可达的局部门与纠缠层排布规则，并在PDEBench回归和Maxwell场建模等任务中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化量子电路(PQC)输出可表示为有限傅里叶级数，可及频率由数据编码门决定，而傅里叶系数取决于可训练门与纠缠门的排布。现有研究虽给出了刻画门结构如何影响傅里叶系数的度量，但未将这些分析转化为明确的设计准则，即未说明应如何排布门才能使目标系数可达。因此需要建立可指导电路结构设计的准则。

Method: 利用编码生成元的adjoint作用，将算子空间分解为按频率索引的二维不变平面，证明每个傅里叶系数恰好是有效态与可观测量在该频率平面对上的双线性投影之和。针对Pauli编码，高频平面由混合多比特Pauli串张成，目标系数仅在局部门旋转与纠缠层使有效态和可观测量同时获得某一平面支撑时才能贡献输出。对于Pauli读出与对易双比特纠缠门，给出在给定相互作用图上排布局部旋转与纠缠层使目标傅里叶系数可达的电路设计规则。使用单编码层，通过排布消融实验、PDEBench回归任务和物理信息Maxwell场建模进行验证。

Result: 单编码层条件下，排布消融实验、PDEBench回归任务以及物理信息Maxwell场建模验证了所提设计规则的有效性；并进一步展示其在中度模拟门噪声与阻尼下具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 论文建立了从傅里叶系数可达性到具体门排布设计的显式准则，阐明了局部门与纠缠层结构对目标频率分量贡献的必要条件，为PQC结构设计与频率选择提供理论依据和可操作规则，并在回归与场建模任务及噪声条件下验证了其有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: The output of a parameterized quantum circuit (PQC) can be expressed as a finite Fourier series whose accessible frequencies are fixed by the data-encoding gates. While the encoder determines which frequencies can appear, the corresponding Fourier coefficients depend on how the trainable and entangling gates are arranged. Although existing studies provide metrics for characterizing how gate structure affects Fourier coefficients, they do not translate these analyses into an explicit design criterion specifying how gates should be arranged to make a target coefficient reachable. In this paper, we provide such a criterion. Using the adjoint action of the encoding generator, we decompose operator space into two-dimensional invariant planes indexed by frequency and show that each Fourier coefficient is exactly a sum of bilinear projections of the effective state and observable onto the planes at that frequency. Because, for Pauli encodings, high-frequency planes are spanned by mixed multi-qubit Pauli strings, a target coefficient can contribute to the output only when local rotations and entangling layers are arranged so that both the effective state and observable acquire support on one of its planes. For Pauli readouts and commuting two-qubit entanglers, this yields a circuit design rule that specifies, for a given interaction graph, a placement of local rotations and entangling layers that makes a target Fourier coefficient reachable. Using a single encoding layer, we validate the proposed design through a placement ablation, regression tasks from PDEBench and physics-informed Maxwell field modeling, and further demonstrate its robustness to moderate simulated gate noise and damping.

</details>


### [109] [Electron decoherence in cylindrical holes and circular apertures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35498)
*Cruz I. Velasco,F. Javier García de Abajo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了快电子在金属圆柱孔与薄膜圆孔两种几何结构中的退相干定量理论，结合电磁格林张量与涨落-耗散定理并保留推迟效应，给出退相干概率与弹性相位的解析表达式，揭示其与轨迹、温度及材料响应的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由电子的相干性决定了相位敏感电子显微术的分辨率与衬度上限，而电子在环境中留下可区分的激发即失去相干性；因此需要量化快电子在典型纳米结构中退相干的规律。

Method: 将电磁格林张量方法与涨落-耗散定理相结合，并完整保留推迟效应，针对实际金属中圆柱孔与理想导体薄膜圆孔两种几何求解，其中圆孔采用柱面波模式展开。

Result: 圆柱孔情形得到闭式、方位分辨的解析式，可将路径位置、温度和电导率的依赖解耦；在高电导与高温极限下化为与电导率、电子速度无关且随温度线性增长的普适表达式。圆孔情形下能量损失概率在低频以1/ω发散，而退相干概率保持有限，并在孔径与相关电磁波长可比时达到极大。由于相互作用空间局域，圆孔引起的退相干通常弱于圆柱孔，但在高温和大孔径时例外。

Conclusion: 对于足够大的孔，退相干可主导聚焦电子探针的空间展宽，因此在相干电子束仪器设计中必须加以考虑。

Abstract: The coherence of free electrons sets fundamental limits on the resolution and contrast of phase-sensitive electron microscopy because coherence is lost whenever the electron leaves a distinguishing excitation in its environment. We develop a quantitative theory of fast-electron decoherence in two canonical geometries: a cylindrical hole drilled through a realistic metal and a circular aperture in a thin perfectly conducting film. By combining an electromagnetic Green-tensor formulation with the fluctuation--dissipation theorem while fully retaining retardation, we obtain the decoherence probability and elastic phase as functions of the electron trajectory, temperature, and material response. For the cylindrical hole, we obtain a closed-form, azimuthally resolved expression that separates the dependences on path position, temperature, and conductivity. In the high-conductivity and high-temperature limits, this result reduces to a universal expression that is linear in temperature and independent of both conductivity and electron velocity. For the circular aperture, which we solve using a cylindrical-wave modal expansion, the energy-loss probability diverges as $1/ω$ at low frequency, whereas the decoherence probability remains finite and reaches a maximum when the aperture radius is comparable to the relevant electromagnetic wavelength. Because the interaction is spatially localized, aperture-induced decoherence is generally weaker than that produced by a cylindrical hole, except for sufficiently large apertures at high temperature. Finally, we show that, for sufficiently large holes, decoherence can dominate the spatial broadening of a focused electron probe and must therefore be incorporated into the design of coherent electron-beam instruments.

</details>


### [110] [How fast can a parent estimate the value of their children? A quantum algorithm for stochastic games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35511)
*Marien Raat,Merlin Incerti-Medici,James R. Wootton,Evert van Nieuwenburg,Daniel Bultrini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出随机二人博弈（expectiminimax 树）的量子算法，以叶子值为查询对象，估计博弈值至均方根误差 ε，查询复杂度为 O(K^D deg^{D/4} (N/ε) D^{4D} log(...)^{4D})，深度为常数时约化至 Õ(deg^{m/2} ε^{-1})，较经典 Õ(deg^m ε^{-2}) 在分支因子与精度上均获二次加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典算法求解随机博弈需 O(deg^m ε^{-2})，其中对抗树的二次加速（分支因子）与单次期望的二次加速（精度）此前分别独立已知；本文动机是验证这两类量子加速在对抗层与机会层相互嵌套时能否同时保留。

Method: 对机会层使用去随机化的多层蒙特卡洛估计器，对对抗层使用相干二分搜索；通过引入均方根误差与均匀误差之间的转换，将二者组合，并与量子均值估计嵌套；适用于父节点值为子节点值的 Lipschitz 函数、机会顶点值对子节点线性的情形，只要确定性步骤具有亚线性查询复杂度的量子子程序。

Result: 查询复杂度为 O(K^D deg^{D/4} (N/ε) D^{4D} log(deg N/ε)^{4D})（D=2m）；若视深度 D 为常数，则复杂度为 Õ(deg^{m/2} ε^{-1})，经典对应为 Õ(deg^m ε^{-2})，即在分支因子和精度两方面同时实现二次加速。

Conclusion: 对抗树中分支因子的量子二次加速与单次期望中精度的量子二次加速，在两层相互嵌套时均可保持；该算法虽针对随机博弈陈述，但可推广至满足 Lipschitz 与线性条件的更一般递归结构，加速性取决于确定性步骤量子子程序的亚线性查询复杂度。

Abstract: We give a quantum algorithm for stochastic $2$-player games. Their game trees are the expectiminimax trees of classical game search: $m$ adversarial layers alternate with $m$ chance layers, every node has $deg$ children, and the leaf values lie in an interval of length $N$. The algorithm estimates the value of the game to root mean square error $ε$ with \[ O\!\left( K^{D}\, deg^{\frac{D}{4}} \, \frac{N}ε \, D^{4D} \log\left( \frac{deg N}ε \right)^{4D} \right), \qquad D = 2m \] queries to the leaf values, where $K$ is an absolute constant. If we treat the depth $D$ as a constant as is sometimes done in the literature, the bound is $\widetilde O(deg^{m/2}ε^{-1})$ against the classical $\widetilde O(deg^{m}ε^{-2})$. So the quadratic speedup in the branching factor known for adversarial trees and the quadratic speedup in the accuracy known for a single expectation both survive when we nest one inside the other.
  We use a derandomised multilevel Monte Carlo estimator for chance layers and a coherent binary search for adversarial layers. The composition is done by introducing a conversion between root-mean-square and uniform error guarantees, and a composition to nest it with quantum mean estimation. We state the algorithm for stochastic games, but it is valid for the following conditions: the value of a parent is a Lipschitz function of the values of its children and the value at a chance vertex is linear in its children, however the speedup is preserved only if the deterministic step has a quantum subroutine with sublinear query complexity.

</details>


### [111] [Induced Coherence in Quantum and Classical Interferometry: Origin and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35523)
*Balakrishnan Viswanathan,Ravi Kamal Pandey,Devendra Kumar Mishra,Sibasish Ghosh,Prasanta K. Panigrahi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子诱导相干干涉仪中验证了相干性、可预测性与纠缠的三重关系，并揭示路径不可区分性对干涉与纠缠的互补调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往研究已在满足Englert-Greenberger-Yasin不等式的杨氏双缝干涉中揭示出涉及相干性、可预测性与纠缠的普适三重关系（triality），但其在诱导相干干涉仪中的适用性及相干性与纠缠的来源和调控机制尚不明确。

Method: 在量子诱导相干干涉仪框架下进行理论分析，显式验证三重关系，并对比经典构型；通过考察路径不可区分性（或称源的固有不可区分性）对干涉与纠缠的影响，分析相干性与纠缠的起源及调控手段。

Result: 三重关系在量子诱导相干干涉仪中持续成立，而经典构型则退化为广为人知的对偶性不等式；路径不可区分性对干涉与纠缠均产生互补性影响；经典与量子诱导相干干涉仪中相干性的性质得到明确阐释。

Conclusion: 诱导相干干涉仪可作为研究波粒二象性及其向三重关系拓展的普适平台，路径不可区分性是调控相干性与纠缠的核心资源。

Abstract: The wave-particle duality that manifests in the Young's double-slit interferometry satisfying the well known Englert-Greenberger-Yasin inequality has recently been shown to obey a universal triality relation involving coherence, predictability and rather surprisingly entanglement. In the present work, we explicitly demonstrate the persistence of the triality relation in the quantum induced coherence interferometer and the classical configuration recovers the well known duality inequality. Furthermore, we explore the origin of coherence and entanglement, in the induced coherence interferometer, and means to control them. We show that path indistinguishability (or intrinsic indistinguishability of the sources) in our setup plays a complementary role in influencing both interference and entanglement. We bring out manifestly the nature of coherence in both classical and quantum versions of induced coherence interferometers.

</details>


### [112] [Analysis of asymmetric errors in NISQ experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35535)
*Gil Kalai,Tomer Shoham,Carsten Voelkmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文延续对NISQ实验（尤其是谷歌2019年"量子优越性"实验）的分析，扩展此前关于对称读出误差的傅里叶方法研究，转而系统分析非对称读出误差，并结合真实数据与模拟数据进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作arXiv:2008.05177提出分析对称与非对称读出误差的统计工具，arXiv:2404.00935深入发展了对称情形。本文旨在补充并扩展对非对称读出误差的研究。同时，Fefferman等人（arXiv:2306.16659）指出振幅阻尼门误差会导致样本中零的比例增大，本文希望检验该效应是否存在于谷歌的真实实验数据中。

Method: 将统计工具与傅里叶方法扩展到非对称读出误差情形，对真实实验数据与模拟数据进行对比分析。模拟方面使用IBM和谷歌的模拟器，在n=12、14的量子比特规模下进行。

Result: 在谷歌实验数据的整个量子比特范围（12≤n≤53）内，零和一的占比仅反映所报告读出误差的非对称性，并未观察到Fefferman等人所预测的门误差导致零比例增大的效应。此外报告了n=12、14下IBM与谷歌模拟器的模拟结果。

Conclusion: 谷歌量子计算实验数据中未出现振幅阻尼门误差所预期的零值比例增大现象，其零/一占比可由读出误差的非对称性充分解释，这为评估NISQ设备的误差来源提供了新的统计视角。

Abstract: This paper continues our analysis of NISQ experiments and especially the Google 2019 ``quantum supremacy" experiment. In arXiv:2008.05177 we proposed statistical tools to analyze symmetric and asymmetric readout errors, and the symmetric case was extensively developed and studied in arXiv:2404.00935, where we used Fourier methods combined with statistical tools.
  Here we extend and complement our earlier study and analyze asymmetric readout errors for data from experiments and simulations.
  Our study was further motivated by a paper by Fefferman et al. arXiv:2306.16659, who showed that the effect of amplitude damping gate errors is to have larger fractions of zeroes in the samples produced by the quantum computer. We show that this effect of gate errors is not observed in the Google experimental data, where, for the entire range of the number of qubits, $12 \le n \le 53$, the fraction of zeroes and ones reflects only the asymmetry of reported readout errors, and report on simulations with $n=12,14$ using IBM's and Google's simulators.

</details>


### [113] [Sublinear Copies Suffice for Fidelity Estimation with Pauli Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35547)
*Jayadev Acharya,Abhilash Dharmavarapu,Yuhan Liu,Nengkun Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种估计已知目标态与未知实验室制备态之间量子保真度的协议，在精度ε下所需泡利基测量次数为亚线性o(d^{0.9908}/ε²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子保真度是刻画量子态相似度的核心量，但直接估计通常需要与维度d相关的测量开销。传统方法在维度较大时测量次数过多，希望突破线性乃至近线性的测量复杂度瓶颈。

Method: 设计一种基于泡利基测量的协议：对未知态在泡利基下采样测量，结合已知目标态的经典描述，通过估计各泡利算符期望值来重构保真度，将测量次数控制在亚线性o(d^0.9908/ε²)。

Result: 在精度ε下估计保真度，所需泡利基测量次数为o(d^0.9908/ε²)，相对于维度d达到亚线性，突破了通常的线性测量复杂度。

Conclusion: 该协议以亚线性测量复杂度实现量子保真度估计，显著降低了高维情形下的实验测量开销，具有理论与实验应用价值。

Abstract: We present a protocol that estimates the quantum fidelity, up to precision $\varepsilon$, between a known target state and unknown lab-prepared state with sublinear, $o(d^{0.9908}/\varepsilon^2)$, number of Pauli basis measurements.

</details>


### [114] [Quantum Query Complexity Beyond the Worst Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35580)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Yanlin Chen,Amin Shiraz Gilani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将经典平滑分析框架推广到量子情形,系统研究了平滑量子查询复杂度,证明其可指数级小于经典查询复杂度,并给出对称布尔函数的近似紧刻画及字符串问题的量子加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典平滑分析能解释算法在实际输入上的优异表现,可超越最坏情况分析;作者希望将这一框架引入量子计算,探讨平滑是否能揭示比最坏情况更大的量子加速,从而在更现实的输入上实现量子优势。

Method: 提出平滑量子查询复杂度概念,构建相应下界与上界技术;核心技术包括一个近似紧的量子算法,用于 \varepsilon-近似两个非重复字符串之间的碰撞数,改进了 Le Gall 与 Ng 的结果;并将该技术用于对称布尔函数及字符串问题(模式匹配、编辑距离)的分析。

Result: (1) 存在一个全函数,其平滑量子查询复杂度指数级小于经典查询复杂度;(2) 对对称布尔函数给出了平滑随机化与量子查询复杂度的近似紧刻画,统一了 Beals 等的最坏情况结果与 Ambainis–de Wolf 的平均情况结果;(3) 在模式匹配、编辑距离等字符串问题上,于多种参数区间实现了多项式至超多项式的量子加速。

Conclusion: 平滑分析能够揭示比最坏情况分析更大的量子加速,表明在更现实的输入分布上存在实现量子优势的路径,为量子算法的平滑分析奠定了系统研究基础。

Abstract: Smoothed analysis is a central framework in classical algorithms for explaining the performance of algorithms beyond the worst case, often explaining why algorithms perform well in practice. We initiate a systematic study of its quantum counterpart and show the following results. $(1)$ We show that there is a total function whose smoothed quantum query complexity is exponentially smaller than its classical query complexity. $(2)$ We give near-tight characterizations of smoothed randomized and quantum query complexities for symmetric Boolean functions, unifying the worst-case complexity results of [Beals et al, FOCS'98] and average-case complexity results of [Ambainis and de Wolf, STACS'00]. $(3)$ We study string problems such as pattern matching and edit distance and, in various regimes, give polynomial to superpolynomial quantum speedups. Our main technical ingredients include a near-tight quantum algorithm for $\varepsilon$-approximating the number of collisions between two non-repetitive strings, improving the result of Le Gall and Ng [QIC'22]. Together, our results show that smoothing can reveal larger quantum speedups than worst-case analysis suggests, opening a path towards quantum advantage on more realistic inputs.

</details>


### [115] [QC-Stark: A Multi-Task Benchmark Revealing Capability Dissociations in LLMs Evaluated on Quantum Computing Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35581)
*Pranav Gupta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出QC-Stark基准，用于评估大语言模型在11项量子计算任务上的表现，涵盖电路构建、调试、编译、纠错与仿真，并发现总体排名会掩盖任务间显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型评估多依赖总体排名，但量子计算任务类型多样，单一总体排名可能掩盖模型在不同任务上的性能差异，因此需要一个细粒度、可自动验证的量子计算基准。

Method: 构建QC-Stark基准，包含11项量子计算任务、5个难度等级和5个随机种子，对10个模型进行2,750次评估；采用Spearman相关分析总体排名与任务排名的关系，使用2参数IRT模型验证测量质量，并进行提示敏感性分析；所有任务通过执行自动验证。

Result: 在11项任务中，有4项任务的总体排名与任务排名之间的Spearman相关不显著，表明总体排名掩盖了显著的任务间差异；2参数IRT模型验证了测量质量，提示敏感性分析确认排名在不同提示条件下具有稳健性。

Conclusion: QC-Stark提供了无需人工评估、可自动验证的量子计算LLM评测基准，强调应关注任务级表现而非仅依赖总体排名，并公开代码与数据。

Abstract: We introduce QC-Stark, a benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) on 11 quantum computing (QC) tasks, spanning circuit construction, debugging, compilation, error correction, and simulation. Across 2,750 evaluations (10 models $\times$ 11 tasks x 5 difficulty levels x 5 seeds), we find that overall rankings mask substantial per-task variation. The Spearman correlation between overall and per-task rankings is statistically insignificant for 4 out of the 11 tasks included in this benchmark. A 2-parameter Item Response Theory (IRT) model validates measurement quality, and prompt sensitivity analysis confirms ranking robustness across prompt conditions. All tasks are auto-verifiable via execution, thus not requiring any manual evaluation. We make the code and data publicly available on Huggingface.

</details>


### [116] [Exponential lower bounds for low-degree strategies in position-based quantum cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35592)
*Isabel M. Moreno-Cuadrado,David Perez-Garcia,Carlos Palazuelos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非局域量子计算（NLQC）的资源下界问题。针对对角±1幺正算子族$U_\varepsilon$，证明若其能以常数精度实现且首轮策略对$\varepsilon$的依赖为低次多项式，则至少一个$U_\varepsilon$的实现需要$\exp(\Omega(n^{2\delta}/(\log n)^2))$的资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLQC能否实现指数级资源下界是量子信息领域的核心开放问题，其答案直接关系到基于位置的量子密码的安全性，并与通用量子模拟器最优性、全息量子引力等深刻问题相关。目前已知的最好下界仅为$n$的亚线性级别，亟需新的技术路线来推进指数下界的证明。

Method: 考虑由布尔超立方体元素$\varepsilon \in \{\pm 1\}^{n^2}$索引的对角$\pm 1$幺正算子族$U_\varepsilon$，其作用为$U_\varepsilon|i\rangle_A|j\rangle_B = \varepsilon_{ij}|i\rangle_A|j\rangle_B$。假设$U_\varepsilon$对所有$\varepsilon$均能以常数精度在NLQC中实现，且关联策略首轮对$\varepsilon$的依赖是次数为$O(n^{1/4-\delta})$的多项式。证明工具基于特定Banach空间的几何性质，即其type常数，并结合取值于这些空间的布尔函数的随机估计。

Result: 在上述假设下，证明至少存在一个$U_\varepsilon$，其在NLQC中的实现所需资源按$\exp(\Omega(n^{2\delta}/(\log n)^2))$规模增长，即给出了指数级的下界。

Conclusion: 通过对角幺正算子族、Banach空间type常数及布尔函数随机估计的组合分析，本文为NLQC指数级资源下界提供了新的条件性证明路径，推动了该开放问题及位置基量子密码安全性的研究。

Abstract: Non-local quantum computation (NLQC) consists on the implementation of a bipartite unitary $U_{AB}$ by two cooperating distant players by means of a two-round protocol with a single intermediate round of simultaneous communication. It is a major open question to know whether there exists a unitary $U_{AB}$ whose implementation in NLQC requires extra quantum systems with dimensions ${\rm exp}(n^{Ω(1)})$, where $n$ is the dimension of systems $A$ and $B$. This type of exponential lower bound is essential for security of position-based quantum cryptography, since the capabilities of the adversaries are precisely described by NLQC. It has also been connected to many other problems, such as the optimality of universal quantum simulators, or the expected properties of holographic quantum gravity. Currently the best lower bounds are only sublinear in $n$.
  In this paper we consider the family of diagonal ${\pm 1}$ valued unitaries $U_{\varepsilon}$, indexed by an element of the Boolean hypercube $\varepsilon \in \{\pm 1\}^{n^2}$, i.e. $U_{\varepsilon}|i\rangle_A|j\rangle_B =\varepsilon_{ij} |i\rangle_A|j\rangle_B$. We show that if $U_{\varepsilon}$ can be implemented in NLQC with constant accuracy for all $\varepsilon$, and the dependency on $\varepsilon$ in the first round of the associated strategy is a polynomial of degree $O(n^{\frac{1}{4}-δ})$, then the implementation of at least one $U_{\varepsilon}$ in NLQC requires resources scaling as $\exp\left(Ω\left(\frac{n^{2δ}}{(\log n)^2}\right)\right)$. The proof is based on geometric properties of particular Banach spaces, namely their type constants, together with random estimates of Boolean functions valued on them.

</details>


### [117] [Low-Weight Canonical Logical Bases from Pair-Partition Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35601)
*Koki Okada,Kenta Kasai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为量子比特CSS码构造了完整的规范逻辑基：通过为二进制循环置换矩阵对划分（CPM-PP）校验赋予四元系数，构造并归一化逻辑代表，再展开为二进制矩阵。给出了通用构造、示例及七个二进制码的参数、校验秩与基权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSS码需要明确的逻辑算符基以支持编码与纠错操作，但现有构造缺乏系统化、规范化的逻辑基框架，尤其在同构性与权重性质上难以保证。

Method: 为二进制CPM-PP校验分配四元系数；构造逻辑代表并归一化后展开为二进制矩阵。当两套校验系统在不相交列集上具有可逆子矩阵时，用单个配对多项式的逆对余因子代表进行规范配对，使所得配对张成整个逻辑空间；当配对多项式为系数为一的循环移位单项式时，归一化保持代表的二进制权重。给出一般块维度与CPM尺寸下的构造流程及对应示例。

Result: 报告了七个二进制码的参数、校验秩与基权重。代表样例参数为$[[320,80,14]]$、$[[448,112,18]]$、$[[2048,512,24]]$；三者X侧与Z侧最大校验权重均为10；其规范逻辑代表在X侧与Z侧二进制权重分别为25、27、27。

Conclusion: 该构造为量子比特CSS码提供了完整、规范的逻辑基，在特定配对多项式条件下能保持代表权重，并通过多组实例验证了其可行性与参数性质。

Abstract: We construct complete canonical logical bases for qubit CSS codes by assigning quaternary coefficients to binary circulant permutation matrix pair-partition (CPM-PP) checks, constructing and normalizing logical representatives, and expanding the result into binary matrices. When the two check systems have invertible submatrices on disjoint column sets, cofactor representatives are canonically paired by the inverse of a single pairing polynomial. The resulting pairs span the entire logical space. When the pairing polynomial is a cyclic-shift monomial with coefficient one, normalization preserves the binary weights of the representatives. We state the construction for general block dimensions and CPM size, work through a corresponding example, and report the parameters, check ranks, and basis weights of seven binary codes. Representative examples have parameters $[[320,80,14]]$, $[[448,112,18]]$, and $[[2048,512,24]]$. All three have maximum check weight 10 on both the X and Z sides. Their canonical logical representatives have binary weights 25, 27, and 27, respectively, on both the X and Z sides.

</details>


### [118] [Succinct Arguments for QMA from Collapsing Hash Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35633)
*James Bartusek,Giulio Malavolta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在仅假设collapse哈希函数存在的前提下，构造了QMA的简洁论证（succinct arguments）。核心技术是量子简洁的claw-state生成协议，可将少量量子关联bootstrap为任意多claw-state关联，仅需经典通信，并由此得到单向函数下的量子计算盲委托协议，再经压缩编译器得到QMA简洁论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前QMA的简洁论证方案依赖结构化假设或更强假设，尚无仅基于非结构化"Minicrypt"假设（如单向函数、collapse哈希函数）的构造。作者希望在更弱的、不蕴含公钥加密的假设下实现QMA简洁论证。

Method: 1) 提出量子简洁的claw-state生成协议，用纯经典通信将少量量子关联放大为任意多claw-state关联；2) 相比[Zhang, STOC 2021]具有更优轮复杂度、标准模型证明且更简洁；3) 由此构造单向函数下的量子计算盲委托协议；4) 将其插入[Bartusek, Liu, Malavolta, EUROCRYPT 2026]的通信压缩编译器。

Result: 在仅假设collapse哈希函数存在的条件下，证明了QMA简洁论证的存在性；这是首个仅依赖非结构化Minicrypt假设的此类方案。

Conclusion: QMA简洁论证可在极弱的非结构化密码学假设下实现，核心技术claw-state放大的量子简洁协议亦改进了已有工作。

Abstract: We prove the existence of succinct arguments for QMA, assuming only the existence of collapsing hash functions. This is the first scheme that relies only on unstructured ``Minicrypt'' assumptions, which are not known to imply public-key encryption.
  Our main technical contribution is a quantum-succinct \emph{claw-state generation} protocol that allows us to bootstrap a small number of quantum correlations into an arbitrarily large number of claw-state correlations, using classical communication only. This improves upon the work of [Zhang, STOC 2021], having better round complexity, a proof in the standard model, and being overall much simpler. This yields a quantum-succinct blind delegation of quantum computation protocol from one-way functions, which we plug into the communication-compression compiler of [Bartusek, Liu, and Malavolta, EUROCRYPT 2026] to obtain succinct arguments for QMA.

</details>


### [119] [Learning sparse quantum states from single-qubit measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35648)
*Su-un Lee,Liang Jiang,Kunal Sharma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究稀疏量子态的学习问题，证明仅用单量子比特测量即可高效学习此类态，无需纠缠门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏量子态虽有紧凑经典描述，但可携带长程纠缠，使得仅凭局部约化密度矩阵无法重建；以往方法需借助大量纠缠门提取信息。

Method: 提出基于单量子比特测量的学习算法：在稀疏度 s 为常数、或随 n 多项式增长等不同机制下，分别分析其样本复杂度与经典计算复杂度；对已知单量子比特基乘积构成的未知基情形给出特定处理。

Result: 当 s 为常数时，算法以多项式样本复杂度与多项式经典计算复杂度学习稀疏态；当 s 随 n 多项式增长时，仍保持多项式样本复杂度，但一般不保证高效经典计算；若未知基为已知固定有限单量子比特基的乘积，经典复杂度为拟多项式。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现无需纠缠门即可高效学习具有长程纠缠的稀疏态，且单量子比特测量要求使其与现有量子设备兼容。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning a sparse quantum state, an $n$-qubit quantum state whose density matrix has at most $s$ nonzero matrix entries in an unknown product basis. While such states admit compact classical descriptions, they can carry long-range entanglement that prevents reconstruction from local reduced density matrices alone. Therefore, previous learning approaches addressed such long-range-entangled states using many entangling gates to extract the necessary information. In this work, we show that sparse states can nevertheless be efficiently learned using only single-qubit measurements. Specifically, when the sparsity $s$ is constant, our algorithm can learn sparse states from single-qubit measurements with polynomial sample complexity and classical computational complexity. When $s$ grows polynomially with $n$, sparse states can still be learned from single-qubit measurements with polynomial sample complexity, although efficient classical computation is not guaranteed in general. In this regime, however, the classical computational complexity becomes quasipolynomial when the state is sparse in an unknown basis that is a product of a known fixed finite set of single-qubit bases (e.g., eigenbases of Pauli operators). These results establish efficient learning of sparse states with long-range entanglement without entangling gates, and the single-qubit measurement requirements make our algorithms compatible with current quantum devices.

</details>


### [120] [Optimal performance in a chaotic Floquet quantum battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35655)
*Lakshmija N K,Uma Divakaran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 周期性踢击与相互作用方向无序共同驱动的N量子比特量子电池，无序使系统离开置换对称子空间，从而提升储能；大无序极限下储能可由随机矩阵理论精确计算并趋于混沌，能量涨落趋于可忽略，同时退相干存储阶段中遍历性效率接近1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找能够同时提升量子电池储能与能量提取效率的机制，并理解无序与置换对称性破缺对充电动力学的作用。

Method: 考虑N个均匀相互作用的量子比特电池，从基态出发，在横向周期性踢击下充电，同时在相互作用方向引入无序；随后在退相干噪声下考察存储阶段。

Result: 无序使系统离开置换对称子空间，储能高于无无序情形；大无序极限下储能可由随机矩阵理论精确计算并呈现混沌充电相；储能最大时能量涨落反而减小至可忽略；大无序下ergotropy接近储能。

Conclusion: 无序与周期性踢击的协同作用可实现高储能、低涨落、近完全可提取的量子电池，为优化量子电池性能提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We study a quantum battery consisting of $N$ qubits interacting uniformly with each other. Starting from its ground state, the battery is charged by introducing periodic kicks in the transverse direction along with adding disorder in the interaction direction. Although the battery Hamiltonian along with the initial state preserves the permutation symmetry, the dynamics in presence of disorder during the charging phase takes the system out of the permutation symmetric subspace, resulting to a larger amount of stored energy as compared to that without disorder. In the limit of large disorder, this interplay brings in a possibility of a chaotic charging phase when stored energy can be exactly calculated using random matrix theory. More importantly, we also find that the energy fluctuations decrease and become negligible in the limit of large disorder, which is also when the energy stored is maximum, a condition highly desirable for the optimum functioning of the battery. We complete the battery cycle by considering the storage phase in the presence of dephasing noise. We find that for larger disorder strengths, the ergotropy remains close to the stored energy, indicating nearly complete energy extractability with an efficiency approaching unity.

</details>


### [121] [The power of oracle access: Optimal sample and query complexity of the abelian state hidden subgroup problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35656)
*Yuhan Liu,Jose Carrasco,Jens Eisert,Armando Bellante*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了阿贝尔群上状态隐藏子群问题（StateHSP）在样本模型与查询模型下的紧复杂度：查询模型下达到 $O(\log(|G/H|)/\sqrt{\epsilon})$ 并证明匹配下界，样本模型下则为 $\Theta(\log(|G/H|)/\epsilon)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在寻找具有超多项式加速的量子算法时，输入访问模型往往决定问题复杂度。作者针对 StateHSP——隐藏子群问题的量子推广，目标是识别未知量子态的对称性——研究这一现象。对于有限阿贝尔群，已有傅里叶采样算法使用 $O(\log(|G|)/\epsilon)$ 个态副本，但该标度是否最优一直未解决，因此作者希望厘清其复杂度。

Method: 作者在两类访问模型下研究阿贝尔 StateHSP：(1) 先前研究的样本模型；(2) 新提出的查询模型，该模型更强且操作上自然，允许访问态制备酉及其逆。对查询模型，给出时间高效的量子算法并构造匹配下界，下界在更强的共轭查询和受控查询设置下仍成立；对样本模型，分析副本数的信息论上下界，允许任意集体测量。

Result: 查询模型中算法使用 $O(\log(|G/H|)/\sqrt{\epsilon})$ 次正向与逆向查询，并证明 $\Omega(\log(|G/H|)/\sqrt{\epsilon})$ 的匹配下界；样本模型中 $\Theta(\log(|G/H|)/\epsilon)$ 个副本既充分又是信息论必要。可见 $\epsilon$ 上的二次改进源自对制备电路的相干访问。应用上，得到学习稳定子群、判定非纠缠以及识别隐藏平移对称性的更快算法。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了阿贝尔 StateHSP 在样本模型与查询模型下的复杂度，表明相干访问制备电路可带来 $\epsilon$ 上的二次加速，并给出多个具体任务的更优算法。

Abstract: In the quest to identify further quantum algorithms exhibiting superpolynomial speed-ups, a recurring theme is that the complexity of a problem is largely shaped by the input access model. Here, we study this phenomenon for the state hidden subgroup problem (StateHSP), a quantum generalization of the hidden subgroup problem in which the goal is to identify the symmetries of an unknown quantum state. For finite abelian groups, existing Fourier-sampling algorithms use $O(\log(|G|)/ε)$ copies of the state, but whether this scaling is optimal has remained open. We settle the complexity of the abelian StateHSP in both the previously studied sample model and a new query model, which is a stronger and operationally natural generalization that provides access to the state-preparation unitary and its inverse. In the query model, we give a time-efficient quantum algorithm using $O(\log(|G/H|)/\sqrtε)$ forward and inverse queries, and prove a matching $Ω(\log(|G/H|)/\sqrtε)$ lower bound which holds even in the stronger conjugate-query and controlled-query settings. By contrast, we show that in the sample model, $Θ(\log(|G/H|)/ε)$ copies are both sufficient and information-theoretically necessary, even if one allows for arbitrary collective measurements. Thus, the quadratic improvement in $ε$ genuinely arises from coherent access to the preparation circuit. As applications, we obtain faster algorithms for learning stabilizer groups, locating unentanglement, and identifying hidden translation symmetries.

</details>


### [122] [An Optimal Quantum Linear Systems Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35660)
*Carlos Bravo-Prieto,Aram W. Harrow,Robin Kothari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文确定了量子线性系统问题(QLSP)的查询复杂度为Θ(κ√d·log(1/ε))，同时解决了Berry和Childs的开放问题，证明任意N×N酉算子可用O(√N)次矩阵元素查询以有界误差实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLSP是量子算法的重要问题，此前上界为O(κd·log(1/ε))和κ√d·(κd/ε)^{o(1)}，下界仅为Ω(κ·log(1/ε))和Ω(κ√d)，上下界之间存在显著差距，需要收紧以确定该问题的精确复杂度。

Method: 通过改进量子算法并建立新的下界，作者将QLSP的查询复杂度上界降低并提升下界，使二者匹配；同时利用相关技术分析酉算子实现问题。

Result: QLSP的查询复杂度被证明为Θ(κ√d·log(1/ε))；任意N×N酉算子可用O(√N)次矩阵元素查询以有界误差实现，解决了Berry和Childs的开放问题。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了QLSP的查询复杂度，并对量子酉算子实现给出了近乎最优的查询上界，推动了量子线性代数和量子模拟领域的理论发展。

Abstract: In the quantum linear systems problem (QLSP), we are given query access to a $d$-sparse $N\times N$ matrix $A$ with condition number $κ$, and the ability to prepare a quantum state proportional to a vector $\vec b$. The goal is to output an $ε$-approximation to the quantum state proportional to the solution $\vec x$ of $A\vec{x}=\vec{b}$. Following a long line of work, the best previously known quantum algorithms for the QLSP had query complexities $O(κd\log(1/ε))$ and $κ\sqrt d(κd/ε)^{o(1)}$, while the best known lower bounds were $Ω(κ\log(1/ε))$ and $Ω(κ\sqrt d)$. We improve these bounds and show that the complexity of the QLSP is $Θ(κ\sqrt d\log(1/ε))$. We also resolve an open problem of Berry and Childs by showing that any $N\times N$ unitary can be implemented with bounded error using $O(\sqrt N)$ queries to its matrix entries.

</details>


### [123] [Optimal Query Complexity for Ground-State Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35668)
*Boyang Chen,Minbo Gao,Xinzhao Wang,Shuo Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了在已知能隙中能量阈值时制备基态到迹距离误差ε的最优查询复杂度，给出了期望查询复杂度匹配的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子基态制备（ground-state preparation）的查询复杂度最优性，特别是在已知谱隙中能量阈值的设定下，确定制备基态所需的哈密顿量查询（$U_H$）和态制备预言机（$U_I$）调用次数的紧界。

Method: 设$U_H$为哈密顿量的α-block-encoding，$U_I$为态制备预言机且$|\langle\psi_0|U_I|0\rangle|\ge\gamma$，阈值位于基态能量上方至少$\Delta/2$、每个激发态能量下方至少$\Delta/2$。提出两种算法：其一使用常数精度谱滤波器构造purifier，并在振幅放大中顺序组合，期望$O((\alpha/\Delta)(\gamma^{-1}+\log(1/\varepsilon)))$次$U_H$调用；其二采用精度递增的滤波器并限制总查询数，最坏情况$O((\alpha/(\gamma\Delta))\log(1/\varepsilon))$次调用。

Result: 期望查询算法重复"制备+一次高精度谱滤波"直至成功，达到$O((\alpha/\Delta)(\gamma^{-1}+\log(1/\varepsilon)))$次期望调用，并证明匹配下界；最坏情况界由Somma与de Wolf [SdW26]给出。$U_I$调用界分别为期望$O(1/\gamma)$、最坏$O(\gamma^{-1}\log(1/\varepsilon))$。在$(N+1)$维系统上，当$U_H$期望或最坏调用数为$o((\alpha/\Delta)\sqrt N)$时，这些$U_I$界也是最优的。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了已知能量阈值下基态制备的最优查询复杂度，两种算法分别达到期望与最坏情况下的最优查询界，并证明了相应的下界，完善了量子基态制备的复杂度理论。

Abstract: We determine the optimal query complexity of ground-state preparation to trace-distance error $\varepsilon$ when an energy threshold in the spectral gap is known. Let $U_H$ be an $α$-block-encoding of a Hamiltonian with unique ground state $|ψ_0\rangle$, and suppose $|\langleψ_0|U_I|0\rangle|\geγ$ for a state-preparation oracle $U_I$. The threshold lies at least $Δ/2$ above the ground-state energy and at least $Δ/2$ below every excited-state energy. We give two algorithms that prepare a state within trace distance $\varepsilon$ of the ground state. One uses $O((α/Δ)(γ^{-1}+\log(1/\varepsilon)))$ calls to $U_H$ in expectation; the other uses $O((α/(γΔ))\log(1/\varepsilon))$ calls to $U_H$ in the worst case. We prove a lower bound matching the expected query count; the corresponding worst-case lower bound follows from Somma and de Wolf [SdW26]. The respective bounds on calls to $U_I$ are $O(1/γ)$ in expectation and $O(γ^{-1}\log(1/\varepsilon))$ in the worst case. On $(N+1)$-dimensional systems, these $U_I$ bounds are also optimal when the expected or worst-case count of $U_H$ calls, respectively, is $o((α/Δ)\sqrt N)$. Both algorithms use a constant-accuracy spectral filter to construct a purifier, which we then sequentially compose during amplitude amplification to prepare a state with constant overlap with the ground state. The expected-query algorithm repeats the preparation followed by one high-accuracy spectral filter until success. The worst-case algorithm uses filters of increasing accuracy and limits the total number of queries.

</details>


<div id='physics.atom-ph'></div>

# physics.atom-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Microwave Power-to-Frequency Transduction in a Magnetically Initiated Rydberg Dissipative Time Crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31824)
*Darmindra Arumugam*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 利用室温铷蒸气中磁致里德堡耗散时间晶体的自持振荡频率，实现微波幅度到频率的非线性转换，获得约1 kHz/(mV/cm)的峰值响应度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有射频场测量通常依赖外部反馈振荡器、重复光学光谱扫描或分辨Autler-Townes分裂定标，系统复杂且需外部工程化设计。论文旨在探索一种无需这些外部结构、由原子多体系统本征涌现频率实现射频幅度—频率转换的途径。
