Method: 在室温87Rb蒸气中施加约14 G磁场建立21 kHz附近的磁致里德堡耗散时间晶体（DTC）；用近共振8.325 GHz微波场耦合63D3/2到64P3/2流形，测其引起的DTC自主振荡频率变化；构建自洽平均场模型，将微波驱动的里德堡布居再分布与集体共振条件耦合以解释非线性；并测量高次谐波的归一化传递函数与绝对响应度随阶数的变化。

Result: 获得总基频偏移约6.7 kHz，峰值响应度接近1 kHz/(mV/cm)；场—频传递函数呈强非线性，其非线性转变明显陡于简单二次饱和响应，可由自洽平均场图像定量描述；高次DTC谐波保持相同归一化传递函数，绝对响应度随谐波阶数近似线性增长。

Conclusion: 该方案基于原子多体相互作用内禀产生射频幅度—频率转换，可利用涌现的原子频率实现连续频域传感，无需外部工程化的射频反馈振荡器、重复光学光谱扫描或分辨的Autler-Townes分裂。

Abstract: Microwave power-to-frequency transduction is demonstrated using the emergent oscillation frequency of a magnetically initiated Rydberg dissipative time crystal (DTC). In a room-temperature 87Rb vapor, a ~14 G magnetic field establishes a self-sustained DTC near 21 kHz, while a near-resonant 8.325 GHz field coupling the 63D3/2 to 64P3/2 manifold continuously shifts the autonomous oscillation frequency. The resulting field-to-frequency transfer function is strongly nonlinear, with a total fundamental-frequency excursion of ~6.7 kHz and peak responsivity approaching 1 kHz/(mV/cm). The nonlinear transition is substantially steeper than a simple quadratic saturation response and is captured by a self-consistent mean-field picture in which microwave-driven Rydberg-population redistribution modifies the collective resonance condition. Higher DTC harmonics preserve the same normalized transfer function while exhibiting approximately linear growth of absolute responsivity with harmonic order. These results establish an intrinsic many-body atomic route to RF amplitude-to-frequency conversion, enabling continuous frequency-domain sensing through an emergent atomic frequency without an externally engineered RF feedback oscillator, repeated optical spectral scans, or resolved Autler Townes splitting.

</details>


### [125] [Systematic errors from scalar slowing-down models in transient SERF magnetometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31865)
*Marek Kopciuch,Harini Raghavan,Morgan W. Mitchell*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出SERF磁力计中常用的单一核慢化因子标量Bloch模型在极化幅度显著变化时失效，进而推导出含纵向与横向慢化因子q∥(P)、q⊥(P)的有效Bloch方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约化Bloch模型用单一标量核慢化因子描述电子-核耦合自旋动力学，仅在极化幅度近似不变时成立；当极化幅度明显改变时该标量描述不准确，需建立更精确但仍简洁的模型。

Method: 推导含分离的纵向与横向慢化因子q∥(P)和q⊥(P)的有效Bloch方程，分别支配极化幅度与方向的变化；针对87Rb在典型SERF条件下进行自由感应衰减（FID），并与主方程模拟对比；进一步分析各向异性响应对非共线光抽运及周期性驱动下谐波响应的影响。

Result: 在主方程对比中，动态标量模型在高初始极化下产生最高4.2%的磁场估计偏差；将慢化因子固定为参考值则误差增至10.1%。降低极化可抑制偏差，但会减少FID信号中所含磁场信息。各向异性响应还会改变非共线光抽运，并在周期驱动下引入额外的非线性谐波贡献。

Conclusion: 所提模型保持三分量Bloch描述的简洁性，同时提升了瞬态与驱动SERF磁力测量的定量精度。

Abstract: Reduced Bloch models of spin-exchange-relaxation-free (SERF) magnetometers commonly describe the coupled electron--nuclear spin dynamics using a single nuclear slowing-down factor. We show that this scalar description becomes inaccurate when the polarization magnitude changes appreciably. We derive an effective Bloch equation with separate longitudinal and transverse slowing-down factors, $q_\parallel(P)$ and $q_\perp(P)$, governing changes in polarization magnitude and direction. For $^{87}\mathrm{Rb}$ under representative SERF conditions and undergoing free-induction decay (FID), comparison with master-equation simulations shows that the dynamic scalar model produces a magnetic-field estimation bias of up to $4.2\%$ at high initial polarization, while fixing the slowing-down factor at a reference value increases the error to $10.1\%$. Lowering the polarization suppresses this bias but also reduces the magnetic-field information contained in the FID signal. We further show that the anisotropic response modifies noncollinear optical pumping and introduces an additional nonlinear contribution to the harmonic response under periodic driving. The resulting model retains the simplicity of a three-component Bloch description while improving the quantitative accuracy of transient and driven SERF magnetometry.

</details>


### [126] [Collinear Laser Spectroscopy of the $1s2s\,^3\!S_1\rightarrow 1s2p\,^3\!P_{0,2}$ Transitions in Helium-like $^{11}$B$^{3+}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33056)
*Axel Buß,Konstantin Mohr,Volker Hannen,Zoran Andelkovic,Max Horst,Phillip Imgram,Kristian König,Bernhard Maass,W. Nörtershäuser,Simon Rausch,Rodolfo Sánchez,Christian Weinheimer*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该文报道了 ^{11}B^{3+} 类氦离子 1s2s ³S₁ → 1s2p ³P₀,₂ 精细结构跃迁的超精细结构测量，采用EBIT产生离子并利用共线激光谱技术，在低统计量条件下确认并改进了先前测量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验类氦高电荷态离子的精细结构与超精细结构理论计算，并通过高精度测量获取 ^{11}B^{3+} ³P₀,₂ 能级的超精细参数，以解决理论与实验之间可能存在的偏差。

Method: 在电子束离子阱（EBIT）中产生 ^{11}B^{3+} 类氦离子，经15 ms短生产周期后引出，采用共线激光谱技术测量 1s2s ³S₁ → 1s2p ³P₀,₂ 精细结构跃迁；处理了离子束团复杂的能量-时间分布以及谱线对EBIT起始电位的敏感性等实验难点。

Result: 尽管存在实验复杂性和低统计量的限制，仍确认并改进了此前对这些跃迁的测量结果；³P₀,₂ 能级的超精细参数与先前结果存在一定张力，但精细结构能级能量与实验及理论均符合较好。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了EBIT结合共线激光谱用于类氦离子超精细结构测量的可行性，精细结构能级能量与理论一致，但超精细参数仍需进一步高统计量实验以澄清差异。

Abstract: We report on hyperfine structure measurements of the $1s2s\,{}^{3\!}S_1 \rightarrow 1s2p\,{}^{3\!}P_{0,2}$ fine-structure transitions in the spectrum of $^{11}$B$^{3+}$ ions. The helium-like ions were created in an electron beam ion trap (EBIT) and ejected after a short production period of 15 ms. A challenge of the experiment was the treatment of the complex energy-time-profile of the created ion bunches and the sensitivity of the collinear laser spectroscopy to the starting potential inside the EBIT. Despite these complications and the low statistics of the experiment, we were able to confirm and improve the results of a previous measurement of these transitions. While we find moderate tension in the results for the hyperfine parameters of the $^{3\!}P_{0,2}$ levels, their fine-structure level energies are in reasonable agreement between experiments and with theory.

</details>


### [127] [Hyperfine structure splitting for the triplet $2^{3}S(L = 0)-$state in the two-muonic helium ${}^{3}$He $μ_{2}$ atom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33059)
*Alexei M. Frolov*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该文高精度计算了双μ子氦${}^{3}$He $\mu_{2}$原子中三重态$2^{3}S$（L=0）的超精细结构劈裂，给出结果为$\Delta \approx 5.1623403103010 \times 10^{7}$ MHz，标准不确定度为$\pm 10$ Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三重态$2^{3}S$态的超精细（自旋-自旋）相互作用源于三重态双μ子壳层与中心氦核非零自旋之间的耦合，需要高精度确定其超精细结构劈裂。

Method: 基于三重态双μ子壳层与中心氦核非零自旋之间的超精细（自旋-自旋）相互作用，对${}^{3}$He $\mu_{2}$原子$2^{3}S$态的超精细结构劈裂进行计算。

Result: 超精细结构劈裂为$\Delta \approx 5.1623403103010 \times 10^{7}$ MHz，标准不确定度评估为$\pm 10$ Hz，达到很高精度。

Conclusion: 该工作以极高精度确定了${}^{3}$He $\mu_{2}$原子三重态$2^{3}S$态的超精细结构劈裂，为相关精密测量和理论检验提供了参考值。

Abstract: The hyperfine structure splitting for the triplet $2^{3}S(L = 0)-$state in the two-muonic helium ${}^{3}$He $μ_{2}$ atom is determined to high accuracy. Such a structure arises as a result of hyperfine (or spin-spin) interaction between the triplet two-muonic shell and non-zero spin of the central helicon nucleus. Our result for the hyperfine structure splitting is $Δ\approx 5.1623403103010 \cdot 10^{7}$ $MHz$, where the standard uncertainty has been evaluated as $\pm 10$ $Hz$.

</details>


### [128] [Non-dipole effects in a multielectron atom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33494)
*Alexey N. Hopersky,Alexey M. Nadolinsky,Rustam V. Koneev*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 建立了多电子原子偶极辐射跃迁算符的解析结构，包含非偶极效应，并指出结果可借助不可约张量算符理论推广至更高多极。 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多电子原子中考虑非偶极效应，需要对偶极辐射跃迁算符的解析结构进行系统描述，并总结已有相关结果。 

Method: 从解析角度构建偶极辐射跃迁算符的结构，纳入非偶极效应；与连续谱“分裂”电子近似下的已有结果进行比较与总结；结合不可约张量算符理论方法处理完整多极辐射跃迁算符。 

Result: 给出了考虑非偶极效应的偶极辐射跃迁算符解析结构，并总结/印证了此前在连续谱“分裂”电子近似下的结果；给出了完整多极辐射跃迁算符的公式（见式(10)），且该结果可在不可约张量算符理论框架内进一步精确化并推广到更高多极。 

Conclusion: 该工作为多电子原子辐射跃迁算符提供了可推广的解析框架，尤其为包含非偶极效应及更高多极情形的研究奠定基础。 

Abstract: This Letter establishes the analytical structure of the dipole radiation transition operator, taking into account non-dipole effects for a multielectron atom. The results summarize those of Komninos Y. et al., Phys. Rev. A 86, 023420 (2012), obtained in an approximation of a "split" electron of a continuous spectrum. The result for the complete operator of a multipole radiation transition [see formula (10)] can be specified within the framework of the methods of the theory of irreducible tensor operators and for higher multipoles.

</details>


### [129] [Breit interaction in elastic scattering of vortex electron on Hydrogen atomic target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34578)
*Pengcheng Zhao*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究300 keV涡旋电子被氢原子靶弹性散射，重点考察Breit相互作用的影响。发现当涡旋电子总角动量足够大时，Breit相互作用在某些运动学区域可主导散射过程，打破低Z原子中Breit作用可忽略的传统观点，且靶尺寸越小越利于观测该效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子涡旋束与原子相互作用是其应用的基础，但现有弹性散射研究仅考虑库仑相互作用，代表两电子间库仑势领头相对论修正的Breit相互作用尚未被系统考察。

Method: 以300 keV涡旋电子对氢原子靶的弹性散射为对象，对比纯库仑贡献与库仑加入Breit贡献两种情形，并进一步比较单原子、介观和宏观靶三种情况。

Result: 当涡旋电子总角动量足够大时，Breit相互作用在某些运动学区域主导散射过程，打破低Z原子中Breit相互作用对电子散射贡献很小的传统认知。其原因是大角动量涡旋电子具有显著磁矩，而Breit相互作用恰好描述磁矩间相互作用。靶尺寸显著影响Breit诱导效应的可观测性，较小靶尺寸有利于观测。

Conclusion: Breit相互作用在特定条件下可显著影响涡旋电子弹性散射，且其效应可观测性强烈依赖靶尺寸，需在相应运动学与靶条件下加以考虑。

Abstract: The interaction of electron vortex beams with atoms is fundamental to their applications, yet existing studies on elastic scattering have considered only the Coulomb interaction. The role of the Breit interaction, which represents the leading relativistic correction to the Coulomb potential between two electrons, has not been systematically examined. In this paper, we study the elastic scattering of a \(300\) keV vortex electron by a hydrogen atomic target, with emphasis on the role of the Breit interaction. Comparisons are made between two situations: the pure Coulomb contribution and the sum of the Coulomb and Breit contributions. Numerous results show that the Breit interaction dominates the scattering process in certain kinematic regions when the total angular momentum of vortex electron is sufficiently large, which breaks with the conventional notion that the Breit interaction has little contribution to electron scattering for low-\(Z\) atoms. This phenomenon arises primarily because a vortex electron with large angular momentum possesses a substantial magnetic moment, and the Breit interaction precisely describes the interaction between magnetic moments. Moreover, we further compare the single-atom, mesoscopic, and macroscopic target cases, and show that the target size significantly influences the observability of the Breit-induced effects, with a smaller target size being favorable for observing this phenomenon.

</details>


### [130] [Interactions and dynamics of metastable triplet-state helium dimer with Ne and Ar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34713)
*Dibyendu Sardar,François Lique*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 首次理论研究了He₂(a³Σᵤ⁺)+Ne范德华复合物的相互作用与碰撞动力学，获得了浅势阱PES并揭示了精细结构守恒的倾向规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: He₂(a³Σᵤ⁺)是直接激光冷却与精密测量的候选分子，但其与惰性气体原子的相互作用与碰撞在超冷及冷能域尚无研究，缺乏基准势能面与散射数据。

Method: 对基态X³A′采用自旋限制的CCSD(T)方法计算相互作用势，构建三维振动平均势能面；基于该面进行包含He₂精细结构能级的完全密耦合散射计算，分析低能散射与准束缚态共振；并对比He₂+Ar体系。

Result: 相互作用势阱极浅，深度仅4.612 cm⁻¹；建立了精细结构守恒跃迁的倾向规则；He₂+Ar表现出比He₂+Ne更丰富的共振结构，体现低能散射对势能面与约化质量的敏感性。

Conclusion: 计算的PES与散射性质为He₂+Ne范德华复合物提供了可靠基准，可用于解释未来实验光谱与碰撞测量。

Abstract: Helium dimer in its metastable triplet electronic state, He$_2(a~^3Σ_u^+)$, is a promising candidate for direct laser cooling and precision measurements. Interactions and collisions involving He$_2(a~^3Σ_u^+)$ remain unexplored in ultracold and cold energy domains. Here, we present the first theoretical investigation of intermolecular interactions and collision dynamics of He$_2(a~^3Σ_u^+)$+Ne van der Waals complex. For the ground electronic state, $X~^3\mathrm{A}'$, we employ a spin-restricted coupled cluster method with single, double, and noniterative triple excitations. The resulting interaction potential exhibits an extremely shallow well with a depth of 4.612 cm$^{-1}$. We construct a three-dimensional vibrationally averaged potential energy surface. Using this surface, we perform full close-coupling calculations including fine-structure levels of He$_2(a~^3Σ_u^+)$ to investigate collisional excitation and quenching induced by Ne. We analyze the low-energy scattering dynamics and model resonant features associated with quasi-bound states supported by interaction potential. Propensity rule for fine-structure conserving transitions is established. We additionally consider He$_2$+Ar collisions and compare their energy dependence and resonance structure with those of He$_2$+Ne. The He$_2$+Ar system exhibits a richer resonance structure, highlighting the sensitivity of low-energy scattering to interaction potential and reduced mass. Calculated PES and scattering properties provide a reliable benchmark for description of He$_2$+Ne van der Waals complex and may be useful for interpretation of future experimental spectra and collision measurements.

</details>


### [131] [Potential-energy surfaces of water and its molecular ions up to \texorpdfstring{H$_2$O$^{3+}$}{H2O3+}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35144)
*Giorgio Visentin,Agapi Emmanouilidou*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用基于CASSCF的多种从头算方法，计算了分子离子（最高至H₂O³⁺）在任意外价、内价及芯空穴组合下的势能面，并与文献基准进行对比，筛选出能准确给出键长与键角依赖势能面的方法，为后续水与自由电子激光脉冲相互作用研究提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究水与自由电子激光脉冲的相互作用，需要准确的水分子离子（含多种空穴态）势能面作为输入，而现有文献中针对H₂O³⁺等高电荷态、不同空穴组合的势能面数据及方法评估尚不充分。

Method: 采用基于CASSCF的多种从头算技术，计算最高至H₂O³⁺的分子离子势能面，涵盖外价、内价和芯空穴的任意组合；将结果与文献基准对比，以评估方法精度；势能面表示为两个键长及键角的函数。

Result: 识别出能够准确生成双键长与键角依赖势能面的CASSCF类方法；所计算势能面覆盖多种空穴组合，且方法精度通过与文献基准的对比得到验证。

Conclusion: 所得势能面将用于未来水与自由电子激光脉冲相互作用的研究；本文针对水采用的这套方法亦可推广至其他三原子分子势能面的获取。

Abstract: We compute the potential-energy surfaces for molecular ions up to H$_2$O$^{3+}$ with any combinations of outer-valence, inner-valence and core holes. To obtain the potential-energy surfaces, we consider several ab initio techniques based on the CASSCF method and benchmark them with respect to the literature. We thus identify the techniques which produce accurate potential-energy surfaces as a function of the two bond lengths and the angle between the two bond lengths. These potential-energy surfaces will be used in future studies of the interaction of water with free-electron laser pulses. Furthermore, the techniques employed in this work in the context of water can be used to obtain the potential-energy surfaces of other triatomic molecules.

</details>


### [132] [Reconstructing Molecular-Ion Quantum States with Two-Color Strong-Field Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35283)
*Dina S. Eissa,Felipe Morales,Abraham Camacho Garibay,Vyacheslav Leshchenko,Cosmin I. Blaga,Misha Ivanov,Pierre Agostini,Maria Richter,Louis F. DiMauro*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"双色强场散射"方法，用强中红外场电离分子并发射电子，弱二次谐波场在电子连续态运动期间共振耦合母离子振动态，通过扫描双色相对相位调控离子演化并映射为亚埃键长变化，以N₂为例探测电离通道间相干性并重构初始离子叠加态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强场电离分子可制备离子态的相干叠加，其振幅与相对相位决定后续超快动力学，但实验上获取这些信息仍是难题，亟需一种能探测和表征离子量子波包复振幅的结构性方法。

Method: 双色强场散射：强中红外场电离分子并发射电子，弱二次谐波场在电子连续态运动期间共振耦合母离子振动态，在亚激光周期时间尺度引入结构变化；扫描两色相对相位以调控离子演化，并从重散射信号中反演相位依赖的亚埃键长变化；以N₂为对象探测不同电离通道间相干性并重构初始离子叠加态。

Result: 在N₂上应用双色装置，成功探测到不同电离通道间的相干性，并重构出初始离子叠加态；相对相位可调控离子演化，并将其映射为可从重散射信号中提取的亚埃键长变化。

Conclusion: 双色强场散射是一种强大的结构性方法，可用于表征离子量子波包的复振幅，为研究强场电离制备的相干离子态及其超快动力学提供了新途径。

Abstract: Strong-field ionization of molecules can prepare coherent superpositions of ionic states whose amplitudes and relative phases govern the ensuing ultrafast dynamics, but accessing this information experimentally remains a challenge. Here we introduce two-color strong-field scattering, in which a strong mid-infrared field ionizes the molecule and launches an electron, while a weak second harmonic field resonantly couples vibronic states of the parent ion during the electron's continuum excursion, thereby introducing structural changes on the sub-laser-cycle timescale. Scanning the relative phase between the two colors controls the ionic evolution and maps it onto phase-dependent sub-angstrom bond-length changes retrieved from the rescattering signal. Using the example of N$_2$, we apply the two-color setup to probe the coherence between different ionization channels and to reconstruct the initial ionic superposition. Our results establish two-color strong-field scattering as a powerful structural approach to characterize the complex amplitudes of ion quantum wavepackets.

</details>


### [133] [Raman Transitions in Collinear Laser Spectroscopy for High-Precision Frequency Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35284)
*Julien Spahn,Hendrik Bodnar,Kristian König,Wilfried Nörtershäuser,Robbe Van Duyse*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文在快离子束中对偶极禁戒精细结构跃迁实现受激拉曼光谱，并将其应用于⁸⁸Sr⁺的S₁/₂→D₅/₂钟跃迁及D₃/₂→D₅/₂精细结构劈裂测量，展示了超越常规电偶极光谱线宽极限的精密共线激光光谱路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规电偶极（E1）光谱受限于跃迁自然线宽，难以对短寿命同位素的窄跃迁进行精密测量；而偶极禁戒跃迁虽线宽极窄、对原子核物理基本量敏感，却缺乏高效探测手段。本文旨在建立一种可探测偶极禁戒精细结构跃迁的精密光谱方法。

Method: 在快离子束上实施受激拉曼光谱，通过拉曼跃迁驱动偶极禁戒跃迁；以⁸⁸Sr⁺的S₁/₂→D₅/₂钟跃迁为对象，结合后续E1跃迁将其转化为可用标准荧光探测的信号。进一步采用无多普勒方案，利用两次相继拉曼跃迁直接测量D₃/₂→D₅/₂精细结构劈裂。

Result: 成功实现了对⁸⁸Sr⁺偶极禁戒S₁/₂→D₅/₂跃迁的拉曼光谱及其荧光探测，并直接测得D₃/₂→D₅/₂精细结构劈裂，验证了该无多普勒双拉曼方案的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明可对短寿命同位素的窄跃迁开展超越常规电偶极光谱线宽极限的精密共线激光光谱，为探测原子与核物理基本问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate stimulated Raman spectroscopy on dipole-forbidden fine-structure transitions in a fast ion beam. It is applied to the $\mathrm{S}_{1/2}\rightarrow\mathrm{D}_{5/2}$ clock transition in $^{88}\mathrm{Sr}^{+}$ and combined with a subsequent E1 transition to allow for its detection by standard fluorescence detection. Building on this, the $\mathrm{D}_{3/2}\rightarrow\mathrm{D}_{5/2}$ fine-structure splitting is measured directly using a Doppler-free scheme employing two subsequent Raman transitions. These measurements demonstrate a path toward precision collinear laser spectroscopy of narrow transitions on short-lived isotopes beyond the linewidth limit of conventional electric-dipole spectroscopy, which can be used to probe fundamental aspects of atomic and nuclear physics.

</details>


### [134] [Qprop-ND: A Nondipole Extension of the Qprop Package for Strong-Field Laser Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35401)
*Haoyuan He,Zhao-Han Zhang,Karen Z. Hatsagortsyan,Christoph H. KeiteL*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: Qprop 3.0扩展引入非偶极效应，可模拟激光传播相关的强场原子现象，并保持原有偶极版本的数值框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有Qprop主要基于偶极近似，无法处理磁场效应不可忽略或激光波长与原子尺寸可比时的物理情形，因此需要引入非偶极修正。

Method: 将波函数按球谐函数展开，采用Crank-Nicolson方法进行时间传播，并使用t-SURFF或i-SURFV方法计算光电子谱。

Result: 代码可用于模拟阈上电离、高频激光场中的电离稳定化、高次谐波产生等强场原子现象，并通过若干示例验证了实现的正确性和精度。

Conclusion: Qprop 3.0成功扩展至非偶极区域，为强场原子过程提供有效且准确的模拟工具。

Abstract: The Qprop 3.0 package for the solution of the time-dependent Schrödinger equation for an electron in a laser field and atomic potential is extended to incorporate nondipole effects related to the laser wave propagation. The developed code can be used for the simulation of strong field atomic phenomena in the nondipole regime, such as above-threshold ionization, ionization stabilization in high-frequency laser fields, high-order harmonic generation, when the effects connected with the magnetic field are non-negligible or when the laser wavelength is comparable with the atomic size. Similar to the dipole Qprop the wave function is expanded in spherical harmonics, the time-propagation of the wave functions is performed using the Crank- Nicolson approach, and the photoelectron spectra are calculated employing the t-SURFF or i- SURFV methods. The validity and accuracy of the implementation are demonstrated through several illustrative examples.

</details>

### [135] [Locality of KMS states for infinite fermionic lattice systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31858)
*Lennart Becker*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无限费米子晶格热平衡态在哈密顿量扰动下的稳定性，证明在指数衰减相关与指数局部不可区分性下，广泛扰动导致扰动态呈现拉伸指数衰减相关，并利用量子信念传播与Lieb-Robinson界限建立相关衰减、局部扰动局部影响与局部不可区分性之间的等价循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无限费米子晶格KMS态对哈密顿量扰动的响应，特别是在相共存（态非唯一）情形下探讨热平衡态的稳定性。

Method: 在被扰动的选定KMS态上考虑广泛扰动；在底层C*-代数上直接建立量子信念传播（QBP），并结合Lieb-Robinson界限分析相关衰减、局部扰动局部影响原理与局部不可区分性之间的关系。

Result: 证明指数衰减相关与广泛扰动下的指数局部不可区分性会导致扰动态的拉伸指数衰减相关；在所述衰减、支撑与均匀性假设下，对迹态扰动闭合了相关衰减、局部扰动局部影响与局部不可区分性之间的循环；在均匀聚簇条件下获得局部平衡期望的可微路径，并线性界定其变化。

Conclusion: 该工作为相共存情形下的热平衡态稳定性提供了新工具，QBP与Lieb-Robinson界限结合可用于刻画扰动下相关衰减与局部不可区分性之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We study how thermal equilibrium states of infinite fermionic lattices respond to perturbations of the Hamiltonian. Our main result concerns extensive perturbations of a chosen KMS state. We show that exponential decay of correlations and exponential local indistinguishability under the extensive perturbation yield stretched-exponential decay of correlations for the perturbed state. Neither the initial nor the perturbed state is required to be unique, allowing stability to be studied in the presence of phase coexistence. Our approach uses Quantum Belief Propagation (QBP), which we establish directly on the underlying $C^*$-algebra. Combining QBP with Lieb--Robinson bounds, we show implications between decay of correlations, the principle that local perturbations perturb locally, and local indistinguishability, closing the cycle for perturbations of the tracial state under the stated decay, support and uniformity assumptions. For extensive perturbations under uniform clustering conditions, we also obtain a differentiable path of local equilibrium expectations and bound their change linearly in the interaction norm of the perturbation.

</details>


### [136] [Local Determinant Defects and Low-Energy Subspace Stability in Semifinite von Neumann Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32095)
*Seyed Mahmoud Manjegani*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个有限温度下低能谱子空间稳定性的检验方法，基于局部行列式缺陷与谱展平原理，在矩阵、有限及半有限冯诺依曼代数框架下给出低能投影间迹L2距离界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种不依赖哈密顿量算符范数接近性的、有限温度下判定低能谱子空间稳定性的方法，以处理下界仿射哈密顿量与迹类吉布斯算符情形。

Method: 利用两个吉布斯态α-z Renyi核的局部行列式缺陷，结合半有限冯诺依曼代数中有界正单射算符的谱展平原理，将局部乘积缺陷化为精确双投影公式；并对相关行列式不等式、等式情形与z单调性进行证明。

Result: 得到低能投影间迹L2距离的界；矩阵情形给出主夹角余项之和，量子比特情形缺陷与热隙精确决定基态重叠；同时给出有效低能模型中可观测量的均匀误差界，并构造了无界哈密顿量的无限层示例。

Conclusion: 该测试在矩阵、有限与半有限情形下均适用，且不要求哈密顿量算符范数接近，为低能谱子空间稳定性与有效低能模型误差控制提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We give a finite-temperature test for the stability of low-energy spectral subspaces. The test uses a local determinant defect for the alpha-z Renyi kernel of two Gibbs states and bounds the tracial L2 distance between their low-energy projections. The main tool is a spectral-flattening principle for bounded positive injective operators in a semifinite von Neumann algebra. It reduces the local product defect to an exact two-projection formula. The thermal result applies to lower-bounded affiliated Hamiltonians with trace-class Gibbs operators and does not require the Hamiltonians to be close in operator norm. We give an infinite-level example with unbounded Hamiltonians. For matrices, the result gives a sum of principal-angle remainders. For qubits, the defect and the thermal gaps determine the ground-state overlap exactly. The same estimate gives a uniform error bound for bounded observables in an effective low-energy model. We also prove the determinant inequalities, their equality cases, and z-monotonicity in matrix, finite, and semifinite settings.

</details>


### [137] [The Hamilton--Jacobi and Symplectic Analysis for Extended Hořava Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32218)
*Diana Vanessa Castro-Luna,Alberto Escalante*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 对线性化非投影λR模型（含BPS低阶项与Cotton张量平方高阶项）开展Hamilton-Jacobi分析与Faddeev-Jackiw量子化，分λ≠1/3与λ=1/3两种情形，获得Hamilton量、括号、特征方程与规范变换，并构造辛张量、给出量子路径积分测度，讨论两情形的量子差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作动机在于：λR模型（非投影Hořava型引力）在低阶扩展项与Cotton张量平方高阶项加入后，其约束结构与量子化性质（尤其是λ=1/3这一特殊点的行为）尚需系统刻画，需借助HJ与FJ两种约束/辛方法交叉验证并厘清量子路径积分层面的区别。

Method: 方法上，采用Hamilton-Jacobi（HJ）分析与Faddeev-Jackiw（FJ）量子化两种途径处理线性化、非投影的λR模型，模型扩展了BPS最低阶项及含Cotton张量平方的高阶项；分别针对λ≠1/3与λ=1/3进行分析。HJ框架下导出完整Hamilton量集合、广义括号、基本微分、特征方程与规范变换；FJ框架下构造辛张量并由其识别基本括号，同时由辛张量计算量子路径积分测度。

Result: 结果显示：HJ括号与FJ括号一致，验证了两种方法的一致性；由辛张量得到量子路径积分的测度，并明确了λ≠1/3与λ=1/3两情形在量子路径积分上的差异。

Conclusion: 结论是：HJ与FJ两种分析在该扩展λR模型上相互印证，括号结构一致，辛张量可用于构造量子路径积分测度；λ=1/3的特殊性在量子路径积分层面表现出与λ≠1/3情形不同的特征。

Abstract: The Hamilton-Jacobi [HJ] analysis and the Faddeev-Jackiw [FJ] quantization for the linearized, non-projectable $λR$ model, extended by the lowest-order term of Blas, Pujolàs, and Sibiryakov and the higher-order term involving the square of the Cotton tensor, were carried out. The cases $λ\neq \frac{1}{3}$ and $λ= \frac{1}{3}$ are analyzed. Within the Hamilton-Jacobi framework, we obtain the complete set of Hamiltonians, generalized brackets, fundamental differential, characteristic equations, and gauge transformations of the theory. In the Faddeev-Jackiw framework, a symplectic tensor is constructed from which the fundamental brackets are identified. The HJ and FJ brackets coincide, and from the symplectic tensor, the measure for the quantum path integral is calculated. The differences between the cases $λ\neq \frac{1}{3}$ and $λ= \frac{1}{3}$ at the quantum path integral are discussed.

</details>


### [138] [Absolute and relative invariants for low-dimensional topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32442)
*Federico Manzoni,Domenico Monaco,Gabriele Peluso*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了d≤2维环面上投影值映射在AZC对称性下的三种等价关系（Murray-von Neumann、酉、同伦等价），通过构造对称基揭示拓扑障碍，并用Chern数与Fu-Kane-Mele等不变量刻画绝对与相对拓扑相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受拓扑绝缘体启发，作者希望厘清投影值映射在不同等价关系下的分类差异，并统一描述低维能隙量子体系的拓扑不变量。

Method: 在伪周期性约束下为投影值域构造对称基；对d=1与d=2情形分别分析AZC各类；比较Murray-von Neumann、酉与同伦三种等价关系，区分极小与非极小环境Hilbert空间。

Result: d=1时所有AZC类均存在完全周期对称基；d=2时出现由Z（Chern数）或Z2（Fu-Kane-Mele）不变量表征的拓扑障碍，作为绝对不变量同时分类Murray-von Neumann与酉等价；同伦等价在非极小环境中退化为酉等价，在极小环境中出现额外相对同伦不变量，其精确值经酉共轭改变，仅相对拓扑相位良定义。

Conclusion: 该工作为低维能隙量子体系提供了统一几何框架，阐明不同等价关系如何分别产生绝对与相对拓扑不变量。

Abstract: [An extended abstract is provided in-text]
  Motivated by topological insulators, this paper analyzes classification schemes for projection-valued maps on $d$-dimensional tori, $d \le 2$, subject to Altland-Zirnbauer-Cartan (AZC) symmetries (time-reversal, particle-hole, and chiral). The main objective is to compare three notions of equivalence for these maps: Murray-von Neumann, unitary, and homotopy equivalence. The analysis relies on constructing symmetric bases for the projection ranges under a pseudo-periodicity constraint, where select basis vectors pick up a phase when looped around the torus. If $d=1$, fully periodic symmetric bases exist across all AZC classes. If $d=2$, topological obstructions appear as non-trivial phases characterized by $\mathbb{Z}$ (Chern number) or $\mathbb{Z}_2$ (Fu-Kane-Mele) invariants. These serve as absolute invariants that classify both Murray-von Neumann and unitary equivalences. For homotopy equivalence, the behavior depends on the ambient Hilbert space: if this has non-minimal dimension, homotopy equivalence reduces directly to unitary equivalence, while in minimal ambient spaces additional relative homotopy invariants emerge. Their precise values change under unitary conjugation, leaving only relative topological phases between maps well-defined. Ultimately, this provides a unified geometric framework for gapped quantum systems in low dimensions, demonstrating how distinct equivalence relations generate specific absolute and relative topological invariants.

</details>


### [139] [Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization for fractional and long-range Anderson models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33084)
*Peter D. Hislop,Rodrigo Matos,Constanza Rojas-Molina*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究长程Anderson模型（含分数阶Anderson模型）的局域本征值统计与动力学（去）局域化，证明在强无序且β>d/2时，本征值统计为Poisson点过程且无动力学局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程跳跃项（如|T(n,m)|∼‖n-m‖^{-(d+2β)}）的Anderson模型中，强无序下已知具有稠密纯点谱及多项式衰减本征向量，但其本征值统计与动力学局域化性质尚不明确，尤其与仅含最近邻跳跃的经典Anderson模型形成对比，需澄清长程情形下的行为。

Method: 针对d维格点上带长程跳跃的随机Anderson型扰动，在强无序区域且β>d/2条件下，通过建立Green函数分数矩的显式、精确下界，进而证明位置算子的大矩发散（无穷），以此推导本征值统计与动力学性质。

Result: 在强无序及β>d/2时，以确定性谱中任意E为中心的局域本征值统计为强度由态密度函数n(E)给出的Poisson点过程；同时证明模型不存在动力学局域化，验证了Disertori等人的猜想。特别地，一维分数阶Anderson模型（α∈(1/2,1)）在强无序下呈现Poisson本征值统计与动力学去局域化。

Conclusion: 长程Anderson模型在强无序下表现出Poisson本征值统计和动力学去局域化，与仅含最近邻跳跃的经典Anderson模型的已知行为形成鲜明对比，揭示长程跳跃对局域化性质的本质影响。

Abstract: We study local eigenvalue statistics and dynamical (de)-localization properties for long-range Anderson models, including the fractional Anderson model. These systems are random Anderson-type perturbations of operators with long-range (non random) hopping terms of the form $|T(n,m)| \sim \|n-m\|^{-(d+2β)}$ for $β>0$, on the $d$-dimensional lattice. In the presence of a strong enough random potential, these models exhibit dense pure point spectrum with polynomially decaying eigenvectors, almost surely. We show that in this strong disorder regime, and for $β>d/2$, the local eigenvalue statistics centered at any $E$ in the deterministic spectrum is a Poisson point process with intensity given by the density of states function $n(E)$. Moreover, we prove that, at strong disorder, there is no dynamical localization for these models, verifying a conjecture of Disertori et al. We achieve this by establishing explicit, sharp lower bounds on the Green's functions fractional moments which imply that large moments of the position operator are infinite. Hence, these models exhibit Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization, that is, the absence of dynamical localization. In particular, this shows that in dimension $d=1$, the fractional Anderson model, a random perturbation of the fractional Laplacian $(-Δ)^α$ with $α\in (0,1)$, exhibits Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization if the disorder is strong and the exponent $\frac{1}{2}<α<1$. This is in stark contrast with what is known for the usual Anderson model that has only nearest-neighbor hopping.

</details>


### [140] [Minimal output entropy for the channels that add or remove a box of a Young diagram, and a Pauli principle for every permutation symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33498)
*Robin Reuvers*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究 $U(d)$ 两个相差一个方框的不可约表示之间的四个共变量子信道，证明相干态输出被所有其他输出优占，从而最小化输出熵，并给出最优输出的 hook length 显式表达及部分信道的全部极小点；应用于 $N$ 粒子置换对称性下的单粒子约化密度矩阵谱界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接两个相差一个方框的 $U(d)$ 不可约表示存在四类自然共变信道（移除或添加方框，并保留新图或该方框即 $\mathbb{C}^d$ 或其对偶）。作者希望确定这些信道的输出熵极小问题，即找出使输出熵最小的输入态及最优输出，并借此刻画多粒子置换对称态下单粒子约化密度矩阵的可达谱。

Method: 对四类共变量子信道逐一证明：相干态的输出的 majorization 优占任意其他输出，从而熵最小；利用 hook length 计算最优输出。对保留方框的两类信道进一步刻画所有极小点，说明极小点不必是相干态，即使为纯态也可能非相干。随后将结论应用于具有给定置换对称性的 $N$ 粒子系统，推导单粒子约化密度矩阵谱的尖锐界与混合态可达谱集合。

Result: 证明四类信道的相干态输出均 majorize 所有其他输出，故相干态最小化输出熵；以 hook length 显式给出最优输出。对保留方框的两类信道确定全部极小点，这些极小点可以非相干，甚至纯态时也非相干。应用得到 $N$ 粒子给定置换对称性下单粒子约化密度矩阵谱的尖锐界，以及混合态可达谱的精确集合；费米子情形对应 Pauli 原理与 Coleman 定理。

Conclusion: 相干态在四类共变信道下普遍最小化输出熵，最优输出可由 hook length 精确计算；保留方框信道的极小点集合包含非相干态，说明相干性并非极小熵的必要条件。该框架统一刻画了置换对称多粒子体系中单粒子约化密度矩阵的谱约束，并将 Pauli 原理与 Coleman 定理纳入同一结论。

Abstract: Two irreducible representations of $U(d)$ whose Young diagrams differ by a single box are connected by four covariant quantum channels: the box can be removed or added, and one keeps either the new diagram or the box ($\mathbb{C}^d$ or its dual). We prove that for each of these channels the output of a coherent state majorizes every other output, so that coherent states minimize the output entropy, and we compute the optimal output explicitly in terms of hook lengths. For the two channels that keep the box we also determine all minimizers; these need not be coherent, even when they are pure. As an application we consider $N$ particles with a given permutation symmetry and find sharp bounds on the spectrum of the reduced density matrix of a single particle, as well as the exact set of spectra that mixed states can reach (for fermions these are the Pauli principle and Coleman's theorem).

</details>


### [141] [Effective atom-dimer bands of three-body lattice systems at strong coupling: exact critical mass ratios and a statistics-quasimomentum duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33576)
*Mikhail V. Dolgopolov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究方晶格上三个粒子在无限强接触相互作用下的原子-二聚体（atom-dimer）能带问题，覆盖三种统计（三同玻色子、两同费米子+第三个粒子、两同玻色子+第三个粒子）及全部总准动量。通过Feshbach-Schur归约得到显式有效哈密顿量，将阈值问题化为由方晶格两点电阻构成的4×4厄米矩阵判据（进而为四次多项式），给出完整原子-二聚体区域与临界质量比，临界质量比介于1与π/(π-2)之间，零准动量处精确为π/(π-2)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅处理三聚体（trimer）和零准动量情形，原子-二聚体扇区在整个布里渊区上尚未被完整分析。本文旨在将原子-二聚体问题推广到全部总准动量与三种统计情形，获取完整能带结构、临界质量比及阈值规律。

Method: 采用Feshbach-Schur归约，将问题投影到配对接触算符的无穷重简并特征值1上，得到具有一阶误差保证的显式有效哈密顿量；阈值问题化为基于方晶格两点电阻的4×4厄米矩阵判据，进而化为四次多项式。辅以晶格求积、符号计算、精确对角化和无限晶格求解器进行交叉验证。

Result: 得到：原子-二聚体区域的闭式表达；临界质量比在零准动量处精确为π/(π-2)；每个准动量与质量比至多存在两个原子-二聚体态；阈值起始律（zone边界处本质奇异性、纯p波中线性/对数起始）；两个精确对偶性表明统计改变等价于准动量平移π；第二费米子阈值的全局范围。所有常数经多种独立方法验证。

Conclusion: 阈值起始律具有重整化群解读：逆晶格格林函数为边缘s波耦合，束缚能通过量纲嬗变产生（晶格标度16），zone边界属边缘（BCS/Kondo）型而非BKT型。该工作完整刻画了方晶格上三体原子-二聚体问题在布里渊区上的普适结构。

Abstract: We study three particles on the square lattice with contact interactions of infinite strength, for all total quasimomenta, for three statistics: three identical bosons, two identical fermions plus a third particle, and two identical bosons plus a third particle. Previous work treated only the trimer and zero quasimomentum. Here the atom-dimer sector is analyzed on the whole Brillouin zone. A Feshbach-Schur reduction onto the infinitely degenerate eigenvalue 1 of the pair-contact operator gives explicit effective Hamiltonians with proved first-order error. The threshold problem reduces to a 4x4 Hermitian matrix criterion built from the two-point resistances of the square lattice, and hence to a quartic polynomial. This yields the complete atom-dimer zone and the critical mass ratio on the Brillouin zone, with bound between 1 and pi/(pi-2), and closed forms at all symmetry points. We prove: the zones in closed form; the critical mass ratio with exact value pi/(pi-2) at zero quasimomentum, reproducing a numerical value; at most two atom-dimer states for every quasimomentum and mass ratio; the onset laws, an essential singularity on the zone boundary and a linear-over-logarithm onset in the pure p-wave; two exact dualities showing that a change of statistics is equivalent to a shift of quasimomentum by pi; and the global range of the second fermionic threshold. The onset laws admit a renormalization-group reading: the inverse lattice Green function is a marginal s-wave coupling, binding energies arise by dimensional transmutation with lattice scale 16, and the zone boundary is of marginal (BCS/Kondo) type rather than of Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless type. All constants are verified independently by lattice quadrature, computer algebra, exact diagonalisation, and infinite-lattice solvers.

</details>


### [142] [$p$-adic spectral zeta functions via the inverse Stieltjes transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33660)
*Su Hu,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文将前期针对离散谱的p进谱zeta函数框架，通过逆Stieltjes变换推广至连续谱情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: p进谱zeta函数是量子物理中正则化微分算子行列式的标准工具；前期工作仅处理离散谱，需推广至连续谱以覆盖更一般的算子。

Method: 从有界算子的预解式出发，利用逆Stieltjes变换构造广义分布（p进谱分布），据此定义玻色型与费米型zeta函数及泛函行列式。

Result: 建立了上述zeta函数的解析性质、特殊值公式与Stirling展开；证明离散情形下旧框架被嵌入新框架；玻色与费米情形呈现有趣的对称性。

Conclusion: 作为应用，讨论了p进量子力学中任意紧子集上的位置算子。

Abstract: Spectral zeta functions provide a standard tool for regularizing determinants of differential operators in quantum physics. In a previous paper (J. Math. Phys. 66: 083505, 2025), we introduced a $p$-adic spectral zeta function for discrete spectra via a locally analytic interpolation function. In this paper we extend the framework to continuous spectra using the inverse Stieltjes transform. Starting from the resolvent of a bounded operator, we construct a generalized distribution, called the $p$-adic spectral distribution, and define bosonic and fermionic zeta functions and functional determinants. We establish their analytic properties, special value formulas, and Stirling expansions, and show that our previous framework is embedded into the present one in the discrete case. In this approach, the bosonic and fermionic cases exhibit an interesting symmetry. As an application, we consider the position operator in $p$-adic quantum mechanics on an arbitrary compact subset of $\mathbb{C}_{p}$.

</details>


### [143] [Homogenized graphs with spectrally embedded defect states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33907)
*Kirill D. Cherednichenko,Stephen P. Shipman*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 通过将两份高对比度周期度量图同构地嵌入空间并用连接边耦合，实现了可容纳嵌入连续谱中缺陷束缚态的连续介质，并借助Fourier插值在统一函数空间内完成图ODE到空间PDE的均匀化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何在均匀化后的连续介质中产生能量嵌入连续谱内的束缚态，即连续谱中的束缚态（BIC）现象。

Method: 将两份晶格图同时嵌入空间并通过连接边耦合；通过Fourier分析插值，将关联算子表示在统一函数空间中，实现图上ODE到空间PDE的均匀化；利用两耦合图产生具有内自由度的有效介质，分解为两个正交的混合态空间；在临界高对比度下保留微观共振，使一个混合态空间的谱带与另一混合态空间的谱隙重叠。

Result: 在临界高对比度下，一个混合态空间的谱带与另一混合态空间的谱隙重叠，从而在有效介质的连续谱中产生嵌入频率处的束缚态；这些连续谱中的束缚态对ε尺度度量图持续存在。

Conclusion: 该机制可稳定产生嵌入连续谱中的束缚态，其持续性由缺陷区域上约化预解式的O(ε)算子范数估计保证。

Abstract: By homogenizing high-contrast periodic metric graphs, we create continuous media that admit bound states produced by local defects at energies embedded in the continuous spectrum. This is achieved by fitting two copies of a lattice graph simultaneously into space and coupling them by connecting edges. The homogenization of an ODE on a graph to a PDE in space is achieved by representing the associated operators in a common functional space through interpolation by Fourier analysis. The two coupled graphs result in an effective medium with internal degrees of freedom that allow a decomposition into two orthogonal spaces of hybrid states. Critical high contrast retains microscopic resonances in the effective medium and allows a spectral band of one hybrid state space to overlap a spectral gap of the other. This serves as the mechanism for creating bound states at frequencies embedded in the continuous spectrum of the effective medium. These bound states in the continuum persist for $\varepsilon$-scale metric graphs due to $O(\varepsilon)$ operator-norm estimates for reduced resolvents on the defect region.

</details>


### [144] [The equivalence of topological indices for $\mathrm{U}(1)\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetric quantum spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34119)
*Hal Tasaki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Ogata 算子代数指标与 Tasaki 基本 twist 指标在整数自旋链（在位 U(1)⋊ℤ₂ 对称、对称局部唯一有隙基态）下等价，无需平移不变性或矩阵乘积表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子自旋链中的对称保护拓扑（SPT）相由不同拓扑指标刻画：Ogata 的算子代数指标适用范围广但较抽象，Tasaki 的基本 twist 指标物理直观但要求 U(1) 连续对称。二者关系不明，缺少在一般（无平移不变性、无 MPS 假设）情形下的等价性证明与可计算联系。

Method: 在整数自旋链、在位 U(1)⋊ℤ₂ 对称及对称局部唯一有隙基态的共同设定下：1) 利用电荷涨落估计构造半链旋转的强极限并确定其相位；2) 通过精确的群交换子恒等式，将 Ogata 指标与局部 twist 期望的极限联系起来，并给出显式误差界；3) 全程不假设平移不变性或 MPS 表示。

Result: 建立了 Ogata 指标等于局部 twist 期望极限的等式，并给出显式误差界；在已知能隙下界与相关基态期望时，得到指标的 computable formula。作为应用，结合既有 twist 指标计算，在显式唯一性与边界场能隙假设下，确定反铁磁 Heisenberg 链的 Ogata 指标为 (-1)^S。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了两类 SPT 指标，使抽象的算子代数指标可借助更初等的 twist 方法计算，拓宽了适用范围并提供了可数值验证的误差控制，示例性地给出了 Heisenberg 链的指标值。

Abstract: Symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases in quantum spin chains are distinguished by topological indices. Ogata's operator-algebraic indices apply to a broad range of symmetry classes without requiring continuous symmetry, whereas the elementary twist index of Tasaki requires $\mathrm{U}(1)$ symmetry. We prove their equivalence in the common setting of integer-spin chains with on-site $\mathrm{U}(1)\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry and a symmetric locally-unique gapped ground state, without assuming translation invariance or a matrix product representation. A charge-fluctuation estimate yields a strong-limit construction of half-chain rotations with their phases fixed. An exact group commutator identity then identifies the Ogata index with the limit of local twist expectations and gives an explicit error bound. This provides a computable formula when a lower bound on the gap and the relevant ground-state expectations are available. As an application, a previous twist-index calculation determines the Ogata index to be $(-1)^S$ for the antiferromagnetic Heisenberg chain under explicit uniqueness and boundary-field gap assumptions.
  The author formulated the problem and proposed the basic strategy, while ChatGPT made substantial contributions to the development of the proof.

</details>


### [145] [Discontinuity of Continuum Fermion Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34402)
*Oliver Siebert*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究连续费米子系统在配对势作用下的Heisenberg动力学，证明对一大类非平凡配对势，动力学在规范不变CAR代数上不是逐点范数连续的，且CAR代数及其规范不变子代数在动力学下不保持不变（几乎对所有时间）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究相互作用连续费米子系统的Heisenberg动力学在代数层面（C*-代数）的良好定义性与不变性，检验规范不变CAR代数对动力学是否封闭。

Method: 利用配对势作用的连续费米子Heisenberg动力学，在适当的色散关系与外部势假设下，结合逐点范数连续性分析与自动连续性论证。

Result: 对一大类非平凡配对势，动力学在规范不变CAR代数上不是逐点范数连续的；CAR代数及其规范不变子代数在动力学下均不保持不变，且不不变性几乎对所有时间成立。

Conclusion: 相互作用连续费米子系统的Heisenberg动力学不能被定义在CAR代数或其规范不变子代数上，代数结构随时间演化会逸出原代数，凸显相互作用量子场动力学在代数框架下的内在障碍。

Abstract: We study the Heisenberg dynamics of continuum fermions in $\IR^d$ interacting through a pair potential. For a large class of nonconstant pair potentials and under suitable assumptions on the dispersion relation and external potential, we prove that the dynamics fails to be pointwise-norm continuous even on the gauge-invariant CAR algebra. An automatic-continuity argument then shows that neither the CAR algebra nor its gauge-invariant subalgebra is invariant under the dynamics, with non-invariance occurring for almost all times.

</details>


### [146] [Classical BV Pushforward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34533)
*Xin Geng,Andrey Losev,Vyacheslav Lysov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文定义了经典BV推前（classical BV pushforward），并证明其保持经典主方程（CME）解以及BV规范等价性的两大性质，同时给出示例与应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在BV（Batalin-Vilkovisky）形式体系中，需要一种能够将大（总）空间上的经典场论数据约化到较小空间上的机制。经典BV推前正是实现这种约化/约化的自然操作，但其关键性质的系统性证明此前尚不明确。

Method: 作者给出经典BV推前的定义，随后以两个定理形式证明其性质：（1）将总空间上CME的解映为较小空间上CME的解；（2）在孤立临界点情形下，将BV规范等价的解映为BV规范等价的解。并结合示例说明该构造。

Result: 证明了经典BV推前保持CME解集（性质一）；并证明在孤立临界点条件下，它保持BV规范等价类（性质二）。

Conclusion: 经典BV推前是连接总空间与较小空间上经典BV理论的恰当约化工具，其保持主方程与规范等价，可用于相关物理与数学构造。

Abstract: We define the classical BV pushforward and establish its two main properties. First, we prove that it maps solutions of the classical master equation (CME) on the total space to solutions of the CME on the smaller space. Second, we prove that, for isolated critical points, it maps BV canonically equivalent solutions to BV canonically equivalent solutions. We illustrate the construction with examples and discuss its applications.

</details>


### [147] [Scattering Measures and Information Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34721)
*Tatsuro Oda*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将散射实验建模为结果空间上的参数化有限测度，构建Fisher信息几何框架，揭示散射测量中的信息结构、测量核的信息收缩性质及其与最优实验设计、量子测量的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射实验广泛用于探测物理系统，但其信息论结构缺乏统一的形式化描述，尤其在总散射强度变化与结果分布变化的信息贡献区分、测量操作的信息影响，以及与量子测量层次关系的刻画方面尚不清晰。

Method: 将散射实验表述为结果空间上的参数化有限测度；利用Fisher度量定义信息几何；将散射测度分解为总质量与归一化形状以分离信息来源；用概率或次概率核对测量过程建模，并分析其Fisher信息收缩性。

Result: 散射测度的分解将Fisher信息分为总散射强度变化贡献与结果再分布贡献；测量核作为实验操作会收缩Fisher信息，从而将一类实验操作纳入统一信息论框架；该表述连接散射实验与最优实验设计，并自然对应不完备探测的量子测量。

Conclusion: 有限测度结构为散射实验提供了统一的信息几何描述，并将散射测量、最优实验设计与量子测量纳入同一信息论层级框架。

Abstract: We formulate scattering experiments in terms of parameterized finite measures on outcome spaces and develop the associated information geometry defined by the Fisher metric. The decomposition of a scattering measure into total mass and normalized shape separates Fisher information into contributions from changes in overall scattering strength and redistribution among outcomes. Measurement processes are represented by probability or sub-probability kernels acting on the scattering measure. Such kernels contract Fisher information, placing a broad class of experimental operations within a common information-theoretic framework. This formulation connects scattering experiments to the theory of optimal experimental design through the Fisher geometry of scattering measures. The same finite-measure structure arises naturally in quantum measurements with incomplete detection, clarifying its place in the hierarchy from quantum measurement to classical outcome statistics.

</details>


### [148] [Notes on Magnetostatic Interactions in $\mathbb R^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34766)
*Vladimir Gol'dshtein,Wolfgang H. Müller,Reuven Segev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出静磁学的紧凑表述：不依赖本构关系，用电流密度势场描述磁偶极分布，通过虚功原理导出机械力泛函，得到Lorentz力、Kelvin力、非对称主动应力场（其反对称部分为力偶密度）及压力项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静磁学处理通常依赖本构关系（包括以太关系），作者希望在不使用这些关系的前提下建立更一般、紧凑的磁偶极受力描述框架。

Method: 以电流密度势场刻画磁偶极分布，给出其在矢量势场中的势能表达式；假设偶极分布虚运动时场做的虚功等于势能时间导数的负值，由此推导机械力泛函。

Result: 导出的力泛函包含Lorentz力密度、Kelvin力密度，以及一个非对称主动应力场（其反对称部分对应力偶密度）和一个压力项。

Conclusion: 该紧凑表述无需本构关系即可给出磁偶极分布的机械力泛函，统一了Lorentz力、Kelvin力、力偶与压力效应，为静磁学提供了一种更一般的变分框架。

Abstract: A compact formulation of magnetostatics is presented. Without using constitutive relations, including the aether relations, an expression is proposed for the potential energy of a region carrying a distribution of magnetic dipoles under a vector potential field, where the dipole distribution is specified by a current density potential field. Assuming that during a virtual motion of the dipole distribution the virtual work expended by the field is equal to minus the time derivative of the potential energy, an expression for the mechanical force functional is derived. In addition to the Lorentz and Kelvin force densities, the force functional contains an asymmetric active stress field, the skew-symmetric part of which is the mechanical couple density, and a pressure term.

</details>


### [149] [A renormalized Lee Model: resolvent and spectral analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34980)
*Claudio Cacciapuoti,Federica Muscolino,Diego Noja,Andrea Posilicano*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造并分析三维质量区重整化Lee模型，通过抽象方法定义重整化Hamiltonian（含算子域、作用及Kre\uın型预解公式）。利用总激发数守恒，将谱分析化为对算子铅笔（Weyl函数）的分区研究，建立HVZ型谱公式，并用Birman-Schwinger算子描述亚阈谱及束缚态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维质量区Lee模型的重整化Hamiltonian在数学上缺乏严格的内蕴定义，且其谱结构（尤其是亚阈谱与束缚态）尚需系统分析，本文旨在为此提供严格的算子理论框架与谱刻画。

Method: 采用抽象算子构造法定义重整化Hamiltonian，给出Kre\uın型预解公式；利用激发数守恒将谱分析约化为对显式算子铅笔（作为算子值Weyl函数）的分区研究；借助Birman-Schwinger型有界自伴非负算子刻画亚阈点谱。

Result: 构造了由低激发分区生成的基本谱分量，并得到预期的分区HVZ型公式的包含关系，在前两个分区完整证明该公式，在显式条件下对所有分区证明；证明每个高分区亚阈点谱由有界自伴非负Birman-Schwinger算子描述；在每个至少含两个激发的分区中，当重整化间隙不小于玻色子质量时对任意耦合证明无亚阈谱，当间隙更小时在显式弱耦合条件下亦然；并给出两激发分区存在单束缚态的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为三维质量区重整化Lee模型提供了严格的Hamiltonian定义与分区谱分析框架，建立了HVZ型谱公式及亚阈谱的Birman-Schwinger刻画，明确了无亚阈谱的条件与束缚态存在的充分条件。

Abstract: We construct and analyze a renormalized three-dimensional massive Lee model; its renormalized Hamiltonian is defined intrinsically through an abstract construction which explicitly provides the operator domain, action and a Kre\uın-like resolvent formula. Conservation of the total excitation number reduces the spectral analysis of the full Hamiltonian to a sector-wise study of the spectral profile of an explicit operator pencil, playing the role of an operator-valued Weyl function. We construct essential spectrum components in a sector generated by lower excitation sectors and obtain the inclusion in the expected sector-wise HVZ type formula; the full formula is proved in the first two sectors and, under explicit conditions, in every sector. Below the free or one-excitation threshold, the point spectrum in every higher sector is described by a bounded self-adjoint nonnegative Birman--Schwinger operator. In every sector with at least two excitations we prove absence of sub-threshold spectrum for arbitrary coupling when the renormalized gap is not smaller than the boson mass, and under an explicit weak-coupling condition when it is smaller. In the latter case we also derive a sufficient condition for the existence of a simple bound state in the two-excitation sector.

</details>


### [150] [GR-FM: Geometrically Regularized Flow Matching for SDF-Based Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35006)
*Yuxin Ai,Zhichang Guo,Fanghui Song,Dazhi Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出几何正则化流匹配框架GR-FM，将医学图像分割建模为从初始分布到目标隐式表示分布的连续概率传输过程，以SDF描述目标结构，并结合双调和正则项与Eikonal约束提升空间平滑性与距离场特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割在边界定位与复杂结构保持上仍具挑战：目标区域常存在弱边界、细粒度结构和不规则形状，且高质量图像与精确标注通常有限。现有基于Flow Matching的生成式分割方法主要在状态空间学习速度场，缺乏对恢复表示的空间规律性和距离场性质的显式约束，易导致空间振荡、边界位移及细结构不连续。

Method: 提出图像条件化的几何正则化流匹配框架GR-FM，将分割表述为从初始分布到目标隐式表示分布的连续概率传输过程。不直接建模二值掩码，而采用SDF描述目标结构，将每个像素表示为到目标边界的符号距离，形成连续几何场；使用Flow Matching与常微分方程实现确定性且高效的分布传输。训练目标引入双调和正则项与Eikonal约束，以增强恢复表示的空间平滑性、结构一致性与距离场特性；并分析几何约束下的连续传输过程及零水平集的演化。

Result: 在MoNuSeg、GlaS与DRIVE数据集上，GR-FM在区域重叠度与边界精度方面均取得具竞争力的性能，降低了性能波动，并仅需少量积分步数即可保持稳定的分割结果。

Conclusion: GR-FM通过将分割转化为SDF空间的几何正则化流匹配传输，有效缓解了边界位移与细结构不连续问题，兼顾精度、稳定性与计算效率。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation remains challenging in terms of accurate boundary localization and complex-structure preservation, as target regions often exhibit weak boundaries, fine-grained structures, and irregular shapes, while high-quality images and precise annotations are usually limited. Existing generative segmentation approaches based on Flow Matching mainly learn velocity fields in the state space but lack explicit constraints on the spatial regularity and distance-field properties of the recovered representation. This limitation may lead to spatial oscillations, boundary displacement, and discontinuities in fine structures. To address these issues, we propose an image-conditioned Geometrically Regularized Flow Matching framework, termed GR-FM, which formulates segmentation as a continuous probability transport process from an initial distribution to a target implicit representation distribution. Instead of directly modeling binary masks, GR-FM adopts the SDF to describe the target structure, where each pixel is represented by its signed distance to the object boundary, resulting in a continuous geometric field. Flow Matching and ordinary differential equations are then employed to achieve deterministic and efficient distribution transport. The training objective further incorporates a biharmonic regularization term and an Eikonal constraint to enhance the spatial smoothness, structural consistency, and distance-field characteristics of the recovered representation. Moreover, we analyze the continuous transport process under geometric constraints and investigate the evolution of the zero level set. Experiments conducted on the MoNuSeg, GlaS, and DRIVE datasets demonstrate that GR-FM achieves competitive performance in both region overlap and boundary accuracy, reduces performance variation, and maintains stable segmentation results with only a small number of integration steps.

</details>
